Conclusion: 该方法实现了帕累托最优公平性，效用损失有界，无需训练和标注数据，可同时处理群体公平和交叉公平问题

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across diverse downstream tasks, recent studies have shown that they can inherit social biases from the training data and further propagate them into downstream applications. To address this issue, various debiasing approaches have been proposed, yet most of them aim to improve fairness without having a theoretical guarantee that the utility of the model is preserved. In this paper, we introduce a debiasing method that yields a \textbf{closed-form} solution in the cross-modal space, achieving Pareto-optimal fairness with \textbf{bounded utility losses}. Our method is \textbf{training-free}, requires \textbf{no annotated data}, and can jointly debias both visual and textual modalities across downstream tasks. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing methods in debiasing VLMs across diverse fairness metrics and datasets for both group and \textbf{intersectional} fairness in downstream tasks such as zero-shot image classification, text-to-image retrieval, and text-to-image generation while preserving task performance.

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal OCR: Parse Anything from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13032)
*Handong Zheng,Yumeng Li,Kaile Zhang,Liang Xin,Guangwei Zhao,Hao Liu,Jiayu Chen,Jie Lou,Jiyu Qiu,Qi Fu,Rui Yang,Shuo Jiang,Weijian Luo,Weijie Su,Weijun Zhang,Xingyu Zhu,Yabin Li,Yiwei ma,Yu Chen,Zhaohui Yu,Guang Yang,Colin Zhang,Lei Zhang,Yuliang Liu,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOCR提出了一种联合解析文本和图形的文档解析范式，将图表、表格等视觉元素作为一等解析目标，生成统一的结构化文本表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OCR系统仅关注文本识别而忽略图形区域，导致文档语义信息丢失。需要一种能够同时处理文本和图形元素并保持其语义关系的统一解析方法。

Method: 提出dots.mocr方法，将视觉元素视为一等解析目标。构建从PDF、网页渲染和原生SVG资产的数据引擎，通过分阶段预训练和监督微调训练3B参数的紧凑模型。

Result: 在文档解析基准测试中，仅次于Gemini 3 Pro排名第二，超越现有开源系统，在olmOCR Bench上达到83.9的新SOTA。在结构化图形解析中，在图像到SVG基准上重建质量优于Gemini 3 Pro。

Conclusion: MOCR范式为构建大规模图像到代码语料库用于多模态预训练提供了可扩展路径，实现了文本和图形的统一解析，提升了文档重建的忠实度和语义理解能力。

Abstract: We present Multimodal OCR (MOCR), a document parsing paradigm that jointly parses text and graphics into unified textual representations. Unlike conventional OCR systems that focus on text recognition and leave graphical regions as cropped pixels, our method, termed dots.mocr, treats visual elements such as charts, diagrams, tables, and icons as first-class parsing targets, enabling systems to parse documents while preserving semantic relationships across elements. It offers several advantages: (1) it reconstructs both text and graphics as structured outputs, enabling more faithful document reconstruction; (2) it supports end-to-end training over heterogeneous document elements, allowing models to exploit semantic relations between textual and visual components; and (3) it converts previously discarded graphics into reusable code-level supervision, unlocking multimodal supervision embedded in existing documents. To make this paradigm practical at scale, we build a comprehensive data engine from PDFs, rendered webpages, and native SVG assets, and train a compact 3B-parameter model through staged pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate dots.mocr from two perspectives: document parsing and structured graphics parsing. On document parsing benchmarks, it ranks second only to Gemini 3 Pro on our OCR Arena Elo leaderboard, surpasses existing open-source document parsing systems, and sets a new state of the art of 83.9 on olmOCR Bench. On structured graphics parsing, dots.mocr achieves higher reconstruction quality than Gemini 3 Pro across image-to-SVG benchmarks, demonstrating strong performance on charts, UI layouts, scientific figures, and chemical diagrams. These results show a scalable path toward building large-scale image-to-code corpora for multimodal pretraining. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/rednote-hilab/dots.mocr.

</details>


### [77] [Topo-R1: Detecting Topological Anomalies via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13054)
*Meilong Xu,Qingqiao Hu,Xiaoling Hu,Shahira Abousamra,Xin Yu,Weimin Lyu,Kehan Qi,Dimitris Samaras,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Topo-R1框架，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习）使视觉语言模型具备拓扑感知能力，用于无标注情况下的管状结构拓扑异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑保持方法依赖特定领域的标注数据，成本高且难以跨领域迁移。当部署到无标注的新领域时，需要解决如何在没有真实标注监督的情况下检测拓扑异常的问题。

Method: 1) 开发自动化数据生成流程，合成具有可验证标注的多样化拓扑异常；2) 提出Topo-R1框架：通过监督微调+强化学习（GRPO）两阶段训练，使用拓扑感知复合奖励（类型感知匈牙利匹配、空间定位评分、中心线Dice奖励）来增强VLM的拓扑感知能力。

Result: Topo-R1在拓扑异常检测任务上显著优于通用VLM和监督基线，建立了无标注拓扑质量评估的新范式，在所有评估协议中表现一致优异。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将拓扑异常检测重构为结构化视觉推理任务，通过Topo-R1框架实现了无监督的拓扑质量评估，为管状结构分割的拓扑正确性验证提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Topological correctness is crucial for tubular structures such as blood vessels, nerve fibers, and road networks. Existing topology-preserving methods rely on domain-specific ground truth, which is costly and rarely transfers across domains. When deployed to a new domain without annotations, a key question arises: how can we detect topological anomalies without ground-truth supervision? We reframe this as topological anomaly detection, a structured visual reasoning task requiring a model to locate and classify topological errors in predicted segmentation masks. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are natural candidates; however, we find that state-of-the-art VLMs perform nearly at random, lacking the fine-grained, topology-aware perception needed to identify sparse connectivity errors in dense structures. To bridge this gap, we develop an automated data-curation pipeline that synthesizes diverse topological anomalies with verifiable annotations across progressively difficult levels, thereby constructing the first large-scale, multi-domain benchmark for this task. We then introduce Topo-R1, a framework that endows VLMs with topology-aware perception via two-stage training: supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Central to our approach is a topology-aware composite reward that integrates type-aware Hungarian matching for structured error classification, spatial localization scoring, and a centerline Dice (clDice) reward that directly penalizes connectivity disruptions, thereby jointly incentivizing semantic precision and structural fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Topo-R1 establishes a new paradigm for annotation-free topological quality assessment, consistently outperforming general-purpose VLMs and supervised baselines across all evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [78] [Reference-Free Image Quality Assessment for Virtual Try-On via Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13057)
*Yuki Hirakawa,Takashi Wada,Ryotaro Shimizu,Takuya Furusawa,Yuki Saito,Ryosuke Araki,Tianwei Chen,Fan Mo,Yoshimitsu Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VTON-IQA框架，用于虚拟试穿图像的无参考质量评估，构建了大规模人工标注数据集VTON-QBench，并开发了基于交错交叉注意力模块的评估模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟试穿系统在时尚电商等应用中日益重要，但缺乏可靠的评估方法。真实场景中通常无法获得同一人穿着目标服装的真实图像，使得基于参考的评估不切实际。现有分布级指标无法反映单个生成图像的感知质量。

Method: 提出VTON-IQA框架，包括：1）构建VTON-QBench数据集，包含14个代表性VTON模型生成的62,688张试穿图像和431,800个人工质量标注；2）设计交错交叉注意力模块，在标准Transformer块的自注意力和MLP之间插入交叉注意力层，显式建模服装保真度和人物细节保留的交互。

Result: VTON-IQA实现了可靠的人类对齐图像级质量预测。使用VTON-IQA对14个代表性VTON模型进行了全面的基准评估，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的VTON-IQA框架解决了虚拟试穿图像质量评估的关键挑战，通过大规模人工标注数据集和创新的交错交叉注意力模块，实现了无参考、人类对齐的图像级质量评估，为VTON系统的可靠评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Given a person image and a garment image, image-based Virtual Try-ON (VTON) synthesizes a try-on image of the person wearing the target garment. As VTON systems become increasingly important in practical applications such as fashion e-commerce, reliable evaluation of their outputs has emerged as a critical challenge. In real-world scenarios, ground-truth images of the same person wearing the target garment are typically unavailable, making reference-based evaluation impractical. Moreover, widely used distribution-level metrics such as Fréchet Inception Distance and Kernel Inception Distance measure dataset-level similarity and fail to reflect the perceptual quality of individual generated images. To address these limitations, we propose Image Quality Assessment for Virtual Try-On (VTON-IQA), a reference-free framework for human-aligned, image-level quality assessment without requiring ground-truth images. To model human perceptual judgments, we construct VTON-QBench, a large-scale human-annotated benchmark comprising 62,688 try-on images generated by 14 representative VTON models and 431,800 quality annotations collected from 13,838 qualified annotators. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest dataset to date for human subjective evaluation in virtual try-on. Evaluating virtual try-on quality requires verifying both garment fidelity and the preservation of person-specific details. To explicitly model such interactions, we introduce an Interleaved Cross-Attention module that extends standard transformer blocks by inserting a cross-attention layer between self-attention and MLP in the latter blocks. Extensive experiments show that VTON-IQA achieves reliable human-aligned image-level quality prediction. Moreover, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark evaluation of 14 representative VTON models using VTON-IQA.

</details>


### [79] [Mitigating Memorization in Text-to-Image Diffusion via Region-Aware Prompt Augmentation and Multimodal Copy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13070)
*Yunzhuo Chen,Jordan Vice,Naveed Akhtar,Nur Al Hasan Haldar,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补方法：RAPTA通过目标检测生成语义基础提示变体减少训练过拟合，ADMCD使用轻量级transformer融合多模态特征检测图像复制，在保持生成质量的同时降低版权风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型可能记忆并复制训练图像，带来版权和隐私风险。现有的推理时提示扰动方法（如随机token插入或嵌入噪声）虽能降低复制，但会损害图像-提示对齐和整体保真度。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：1) Region-Aware Prompt Augmentation (RAPTA)：使用目标检测器找到显著区域，将其转换为语义基础的提示变体，在训练期间随机采样以增加多样性，同时保持语义对齐；2) Attention-Driven Multimodal Copy Detection (ADMCD)：通过轻量级transformer聚合局部补丁、全局语义和纹理线索，生成融合表示，并应用简单阈值决策规则检测复制，无需大型标注数据集训练。

Result: 实验表明，RAPTA在保持高合成质量的同时减少了过拟合，ADMCD可靠地检测复制，优于单模态指标。

Conclusion: 本文提出的RAPTA和ADMCD方法有效解决了文本到图像扩散模型的记忆和复制问题，在降低版权风险的同时保持了生成质量，为模型安全和版权保护提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-image diffusion models can produce impressive visuals but may memorize and reproduce training images, creating copyright and privacy risks. Existing prompt perturbations applied at inference time, such as random token insertion or embedding noise, may lower copying but often harm image-prompt alignment and overall fidelity. To address this, we introduce two complementary methods. First, Region-Aware Prompt Augmentation (RAPTA) uses an object detector to find salient regions and turn them into semantically grounded prompt variants, which are randomly sampled during training to increase diversity, while maintaining semantic alignment. Second, Attention-Driven Multimodal Copy Detection (ADMCD) aggregates local patch, global semantic, and texture cues with a lightweight transformer to produce a fused representation, and applies simple thresholded decision rules to detect copying without training with large annotated datasets. Experiments show that RAPTA reduces overfitting while maintaining high synthesis quality, and that ADMCD reliably detects copying, outperforming single-modal metrics.

</details>


### [80] [V-Bridge: Bridging Video Generative Priors to Versatile Few-shot Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13089)
*Shenghe Zheng,Junpeng Jiang,Wenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频生成模型通过少量训练样本即可激活其隐含的图像修复能力，实现多任务统一修复


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频生成模型已学习丰富的视觉先验，但其作为通用视觉学习器的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。传统图像修复方法需要大量数据和专门架构，而视频模型可能隐含强大的修复能力

Method: 提出V-Bridge框架，将图像修复重新定义为渐进式生成过程而非静态回归问题，利用视频模型模拟从退化输入到高质量输出的逐步细化。仅用1,000个多任务训练样本激活预训练视频模型的修复能力

Result: 仅用不到现有修复方法2%的训练数据，预训练视频模型就能实现具有竞争力的图像修复性能，单个模型可处理多个修复任务，性能与专门设计的架构相当

Conclusion: 视频生成模型隐含学习到强大且可迁移的修复先验，仅需极少数据即可激活，挑战了生成建模与低级视觉之间的传统界限，为视觉基础模型开辟了新的设计范式

Abstract: Large-scale video generative models are trained on vast and diverse visual data, enabling them to internalize rich structural, semantic, and dynamic priors of the visual world. While these models have demonstrated impressive generative capability, their potential as general-purpose visual learners remains largely untapped. In this work, we introduce V-Bridge, a framework that bridges this latent capacity to versatile few-shot image restoration tasks. We reinterpret image restoration not as a static regression problem, but as a progressive generative process, and leverage video models to simulate the gradual refinement from degraded inputs to high-fidelity outputs. Surprisingly, with only 1,000 multi-task training samples (less than 2% of existing restoration methods), pretrained video models can be induced to perform competitive image restoration, achieving multiple tasks with a single model, rivaling specialized architectures designed explicitly for this purpose. Our findings reveal that video generative models implicitly learn powerful and transferable restoration priors that can be activated with only extremely limited data, challenging the traditional boundary between generative modeling and low-level vision, and opening a new design paradigm for foundation models in visual tasks.

</details>


### [81] [Reasoning over Video: Evaluating How MLLMs Extract, Integrate, and Reconstruct Spatiotemporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13091)
*Seunghwan Bang,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VAEX-BENCH基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在视频抽象时空推理能力，填补现有基准主要关注提取式推理的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准主要强调提取式推理（答案可直接从时空事件中获取），而缺乏对抽象时空推理能力的评估。抽象时空推理需要整合时间观察、结合分散线索并推断隐式空间和上下文结构，这对具身智能体发展至关重要。

Method: 1) 形式化视频抽象时空推理概念；2) 提出结构化评估分类法，系统定位核心维度；3) 构建可控、场景驱动的合成第一人称视频数据集，涵盖对象、房间和楼层平面图级别场景；4) 基于此框架提出VAEX-BENCH基准，包含五个抽象推理任务及其提取式对应任务。

Result: 通过广泛实验比较最先进MLLM在提取式和抽象式设置下的性能，揭示了它们在抽象任务上的局限性，并对潜在瓶颈进行了细粒度分析。数据集即将发布。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了视频抽象时空推理评估的空白，为评估多模态大语言模型的抽象推理能力提供了系统框架和基准，有助于推动具身智能体和视频理解技术的发展。

Abstract: The growing interest in embodied agents increases the demand for spatiotemporal video understanding, yet existing benchmarks largely emphasize extractive reasoning, where answers can be explicitly presented within spatiotemporal events. It remains unclear whether multimodal large language models can instead perform abstractive spatiotemporal reasoning, which requires integrating observations over time, combining dispersed cues, and inferring implicit spatial and contextual structure. To address this gap, we formalize abstractive spatiotemporal reasoning from videos by introducing a structured evaluation taxonomy that systematically targets its core dimensions and construct a controllable, scenario-driven synthetic egocentric video dataset tailored to evaluate abstractive spatiotemporal reasoning capabilities, spanning object-, room-, and floor-plan-level scenarios. Based on this framework, we present VAEX-BENCH, a benchmark comprising five abstractive reasoning tasks together with their extractive counterparts. Our extensive experiments compare the performance of state-of-the-art MLLMs under extractive and abstractive settings, exposing their limitations on abstractive tasks and providing a fine-grained analysis of the underlying bottlenecks. The dataset will be released soon.

</details>


### [82] [BenDFM: A taxonomy and synthetic CAD dataset for manufacturability assessment in sheet metal bending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13102)
*Matteo Ballegeer,Dries F. Benoit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了BenDFM数据集，这是首个用于钣金弯曲制造性评估的合成数据集，包含20,000个可制造和不可制造零件，并建立了制造性度量的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的制造性预测方法面临两大挑战：1) 制造性定义不一致导致学习目标混乱；2) 缺乏合适的数据集（工业数据集通常只包含可制造零件，而现有合成数据集局限于简单几何和减材工艺）。

Method: 1) 提出制造性度量的分类体系，从配置依赖性和测量类型两个维度进行分类；2) 开发BenDFM数据集，包含20,000个零件，使用过程感知的弯曲模拟生成，提供折叠和展开几何形状以及多种制造性标签。

Result: 1) 建立了制造性度量的系统分类框架；2) 创建了首个针对钣金弯曲的制造性评估数据集；3) 在BenDFM上测试了两种最先进的3D学习架构，发现基于图的表示方法（捕捉零件表面关系）精度更高，而预测依赖特定制造设置的度量更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统分类框架和首个钣金弯曲制造性数据集，为基于学习的DFM研究提供了重要基础，揭示了不同表示方法对制造性预测的影响，并指出了依赖特定制造设置的度量预测仍是未来研究的挑战方向。

Abstract: Predicting the manufacturability of CAD designs early, in terms of both feasibility and required effort, is a key goal of Design for Manufacturing (DFM). Despite advances in deep learning for CAD and its widespread use in manufacturing process selection, learning-based approaches for predicting manufacturability within a specific process remain limited. Two key challenges limit progress: inconsistency across prior work in how manufacturability is defined and consequently in the associated learning targets, and a scarcity of suitable datasets. Existing labels vary significantly: they may reflect intrinsic design constraints or depend on specific manufacturing capabilities (such as available tools), and they range from discrete feasibility checks to continuous complexity measures. Furthermore, industrial datasets typically contain only manufacturable parts, offering little signal for infeasible cases, while existing synthetic datasets focus on simple geometries and subtractive processes. To address these gaps, we propose a taxonomy of manufacturability metrics along the axes of configuration dependence and measurement type, allowing clearer scoping of generalizability and learning objectives. Next, we introduce BenDFM, the first synthetic dataset for manufacturability assessment in sheet metal bending. BenDFM contains 20,000 parts, both manufacturable and unmanufacturable, generated with process-aware bending simulations, providing both folded and unfolded geometries and multiple manufacturability labels across the taxonomy, enabling systematic study of previously unexplored learning-based DFM challenges. We benchmark two state-of-the-art 3D learning architectures on BenDFM, showing that graph-based representations that capture relationships between part surfaces achieve better accuracy, and that predicting metrics that depend on specific manufacturing setups remains more challenging.

</details>


### [83] [NOIR: Neural Operator mapping for Implicit Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13118)
*Sidaty El Hadramy,Nazim Haouchine,Michael Wehrli,Philippe C. Cattin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOIR是一个将医学影像任务重构为连续函数空间间算子学习的框架，通过隐式神经表示和神经算子实现分辨率无关的函数到函数变换，挑战了传统的离散网格深度学习范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前医学影像处理中占主导地位的离散网格深度学习范式，这种范式受限于固定的像素/体素网格，无法实现分辨率无关的处理和连续函数空间间的变换。

Method: 将离散医学信号嵌入共享的隐式神经表示中，学习一个神经算子来映射其潜在调制，实现分辨率无关的函数到函数变换。框架包括信号嵌入、隐式表示和神经算子学习三个核心组件。

Result: 在多个2D和3D下游任务（分割、形状补全、图像到图像翻译、图像合成）和多个公开数据集（Shenzhen、OASIS-4、SkullBreak、fastMRI）以及内部临床数据集上取得竞争性性能，在原生分辨率下表现良好，对未见过的离散化具有强鲁棒性，并经验性地满足神经算子的关键理论性质。

Conclusion: NOIR成功地将医学影像任务重构为连续函数空间间的算子学习，提供了一种分辨率无关的替代方案，挑战了传统的离散网格深度学习范式，在保持竞争性能的同时展现出对未见离散化的强鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents NOIR, a framework that reframes core medical imaging tasks as operator learning between continuous function spaces, challenging the prevailing paradigm of discrete grid-based deep learning. Instead of operating on fixed pixel or voxel grids, NOIR embeds discrete medical signals into shared Implicit Neural Representations and learns a Neural Operator that maps between their latent modulations, enabling resolution-independent function-to-function transformations. We evaluate NOIR across multiple 2D and 3D downstream tasks, including segmentation, shape completion, image-to-image translation, and image synthesis, on several public datasets such as Shenzhen, OASIS-4, SkullBreak, fastMRI, as well as an in-house clinical dataset. It achieves competitive performance at native resolution while demonstrating strong robustness to unseen discretizations, and empirically satisfies key theoretical properties of neural operators. The project page is available here: https://github.com/Sidaty1/NOIR-io.

</details>


### [84] [FDeID-Toolbox: Face De-Identification Toolbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13121)
*Hui Wei,Hao Yu,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FDeID-Toolbox：一个用于人脸去标识化研究的综合性工具箱，旨在解决该领域实现碎片化、评估协议不一致和结果不可比的问题，通过模块化架构提供标准化数据加载、统一方法实现、灵活推理管道和系统评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸去标识化领域存在实现碎片化、评估协议不一致、不同研究结果不可比的问题，这源于任务本身的复杂性：FDeID涉及多个下游应用（如年龄估计、性别识别、表情分析），需要在隐私保护、效用保持和视觉质量三个维度进行评估，导致现有代码库难以使用和扩展。

Method: 提出了FDeID-Toolbox，一个模块化架构的工具箱，包含四个核心组件：1）主流基准数据集的标准化数据加载器；2）从经典方法到最先进生成模型的统一方法实现；3）灵活的推理管道；4）涵盖隐私、效用和质量指标的系统评估协议。

Result: 通过实验证明，FDeID-Toolbox能够在一致条件下对不同的FDeID方法进行公平且可重复的比较。

Conclusion: FDeID-Toolbox为可重复的人脸去标识化研究提供了一个全面的工具箱，解决了该领域的碎片化问题，促进了公平比较和可重复性研究。

Abstract: Face de-identification (FDeID) aims to remove personally identifiable information from facial images while preserving task-relevant utility attributes such as age, gender, and expression. It is critical for privacy-preserving computer vision, yet the field suffers from fragmented implementations, inconsistent evaluation protocols, and incomparable results across studies. These challenges stem from the inherent complexity of the task: FDeID spans multiple downstream applications (e.g., age estimation, gender recognition, expression analysis) and requires evaluation across three dimensions (e.g., privacy protection, utility preservation, and visual quality), making existing codebases difficult to use and extend. To address these issues, we present FDeID-Toolbox, a comprehensive toolbox designed for reproducible FDeID research. Our toolbox features a modular architecture comprising four core components: (1) standardized data loaders for mainstream benchmark datasets, (2) unified method implementations spanning classical approaches to SOTA generative models, (3) flexible inference pipelines, and (4) systematic evaluation protocols covering privacy, utility, and quality metrics. Through experiments, we demonstrate that FDeID-Toolbox enables fair and reproducible comparison of diverse FDeID methods under consistent conditions.

</details>


### [85] [Perceive What Matters: Relevance-Driven Scheduling for Multimodal Streaming Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13176)
*Dingcheng Huang,Xiaotong Zhang,Kamal Youcef-Toumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级感知调度框架，利用历史帧输出实时调度必要感知模块，减少人机协作中多模态感知的计算延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中多感知模块并行处理会累积延迟，影响流式感知性能；现有方法存在信息冗余和计算资源分配不优的问题

Method: 基于Relevance概念和HRC事件信息稀疏性，利用历史帧输出估计场景上下文，实时调度必要感知模块的轻量级框架

Result: 计算延迟降低27.52%，MMPose激活召回率提升72.73%，关键帧准确率达98%，在保持精度的同时提升实时感知效率

Conclusion: 该框架能有效提升HRC中多模态流式感知系统的实时效率，具有可扩展性和系统性潜力

Abstract: In modern human-robot collaboration (HRC) applications, multiple perception modules jointly extract visual, auditory, and contextual cues to achieve comprehensive scene understanding, enabling the robot to provide appropriate assistance to human agents intelligently. While executing multiple perception modules on a frame-by-frame basis enhances perception quality in offline settings, it inevitably accumulates latency, leading to a substantial decline in system performance in streaming perception scenarios. Recent work in scene understanding, termed Relevance, has established a solid foundation for developing efficient methodologies in HRC. However, modern perception pipelines still face challenges related to information redundancy and suboptimal allocation of computational resources. Drawing inspiration from the Relevance concept and the information sparsity in HRC events, we propose a novel lightweight perception scheduling framework that efficiently leverages output from previous frames to estimate and schedule necessary perception modules in real-time based on scene context. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed perception scheduling framework effectively reduces computational latency by up to 27.52% compared to conventional parallel perception pipelines, while also achieving a 72.73% improvement in MMPose activation recall. Additionally, the framework demonstrates high keyframe accuracy, achieving rates of up to 98%. The results validate the framework's capability to enhance real-time perception efficiency without significantly compromising accuracy. The framework shows potential as a scalable and systematic solution for multimodal streaming perception systems in HRC.

</details>


### [86] [Towards Spatio-Temporal World Scene Graph Generation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13185)
*Rohith Peddi,Saurabh,Shravan Shanmugam,Likhitha Pallapothula,Yu Xiang,Parag Singla,Vibhav Gogate*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出World Scene Graph Generation任务，构建包含可见和不可见对象的世界场景图，通过ActionGenome4D数据集和三种互补方法实现时空持续推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空场景图方法存在局限性：仅关注当前可见对象，丢弃被遮挡实体，且局限于2D空间。需要实现世界中心、时间持续、可解释的场景推理

Method: 1) 引入ActionGenome4D数据集，将Action Genome视频升级为4D场景；2) 提出World Scene Graph Generation任务；3) 开发三种互补方法：PWG（零阶特征缓冲）、MWAE（掩码完成与跨视图关联检索）、4DST（可微分时序注意力）；4) 设计基于Graph RAG的视觉语言模型基准

Result: 建立了包含世界坐标系边界框、密集关系标注（包括暂时不可见对象）的数据集；提出了WSGG任务框架；开发了三种具有不同归纳偏置的方法；为未定位关系预测建立了基准

Conclusion: WSGG将视频场景理解推向世界中心、时间持续、可解释的场景推理，通过4D重建和对象持久性建模解决了现有方法的局限性

Abstract: Spatio-temporal scene graphs provide a principled representation for modeling evolving object interactions, yet existing methods remain fundamentally frame-centric: they reason only about currently visible objects, discard entities upon occlusion, and operate in 2D. To address this, we first introduce ActionGenome4D, a dataset that upgrades Action Genome videos into 4D scenes via feed-forward 3D reconstruction, world-frame oriented bounding boxes for every object involved in actions, and dense relationship annotations including for objects that are temporarily unobserved due to occlusion or camera motion. Building on this data, we formalize World Scene Graph Generation (WSGG), the task of constructing a world scene graph at each timestamp that encompasses all interacting objects in the scene, both observed and unobserved. We then propose three complementary methods, each exploring a different inductive bias for reasoning about unobserved objects: PWG (Persistent World Graph), which implements object permanence via a zero-order feature buffer; MWAE (Masked World Auto-Encoder), which reframes unobserved-object reasoning as masked completion with cross-view associative retrieval; and 4DST (4D Scene Transformer), which replaces the static buffer with differentiable per-object temporal attention enriched by 3D motion and camera-pose features. We further design and evaluate the performance of strong open-source Vision-Language Models on the WSGG task via a suite of Graph RAG-based approaches, establishing baselines for unlocalized relationship prediction. WSGG thus advances video scene understanding toward world-centric, temporally persistent, and interpretable scene reasoning.

</details>


### [87] [Out of Sight, Out of Mind? Evaluating State Evolution in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13215)
*Ziqi Ma,Mengzhan Liufu,Georgia Gkioxari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STEVO-Bench是一个评估视频世界模型能否将状态演化与观察解耦的基准测试，通过遮挡物插入、关灯、相机"移开"轨迹等观察控制指令来测试模型在无观察情况下的演化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的演化（如倒水、冰融化）无论是否被观察都会发生，但视频世界模型通过2D帧观测生成"世界"。本文旨在探究这些生成的"世界"能否在无观察情况下演化，即模型能否将状态演化与观察解耦。

Method: 设计了STEVO-Bench基准测试，通过对演化过程施加观察控制：包括遮挡物插入、关灯、指定相机"移开"轨迹等指令。评估视频模型在有相机控制和没有相机控制情况下对多种自然演化过程的处理能力。

Result: 评估揭示了视频世界模型在解耦状态演化与观察方面的局限性。STEVO-Bench提出了自动检测和分离视频世界模型在自然状态演化关键方面失败模式的评估协议。

Conclusion: STEVO-Bench结果分析为当前视频世界模型的潜在数据和架构偏差提供了新见解，表明现有模型在解耦状态演化与观察方面存在显著不足。

Abstract: Evolutions in the world, such as water pouring or ice melting, happen regardless of being observed. Video world models generate "worlds" via 2D frame observations. Can these generated "worlds" evolve regardless of observation? To probe this question, we design a benchmark to evaluate whether video world models can decouple state evolution from observation. Our benchmark, STEVO-Bench, applies observation control to evolving processes via instructions of occluder insertion, turning off the light, or specifying camera "lookaway" trajectories. By evaluating video models with and without camera control for a diverse set of naturally-occurring evolutions, we expose their limitations in decoupling state evolution from observation. STEVO-Bench proposes an evaluation protocol to automatically detect and disentangle failure modes of video world models across key aspects of natural state evolution. Analysis of STEVO-Bench results provide new insight into potential data and architecture bias of present-day video world models. Project website: https://glab-caltech.github.io/STEVOBench/. Blog: https://ziqi-ma.github.io/blog/2026/outofsight/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [A Learning-Based Approach for Contact Detection, Localization, and Force Estimation of Continuum Manipulators With Integrated OFDR Optical Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12347)
*Mobina Tavangarifard,Jonathan S. Kacines,Qiyu Li,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于级联学习框架的连续体机械臂接触检测与力估计方法，利用单根分布式OFDR光纤实现接触发生、位置和力大小的联合推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机械臂在微创手术中广泛应用，但其分布式变形特性使得力感知、接触检测、定位和力估计具有挑战性，特别是在未知弧长位置发生相互作用时。

Method: 提出级联学习框架：1) 使用梯度提升分类器进行接触检测；2) 使用CNN-FiLM模型预测沿机械臂的空间力分布，从而估计接触位置和相互作用力大小。系统仅需单根沿机械臂一侧嵌入的分布式OFDR光纤提供密集应变测量。

Result: 在传感器化肌腱驱动连续体机械臂的受阻环境中进行实验验证，证明单根分布式OFDR光纤能够提供足够信息，联合推断连续体机械臂中的接触发生、位置和力。

Conclusion: 所提出的级联学习框架有效解决了连续体机械臂在未知弧长位置发生相互作用时的力感知挑战，为微创手术中的精确操作提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Continuum manipulators (CMs) are widely used in minimally invasive procedures due to their compliant structure and ability to navigate deep and confined anatomical environments. However, their distributed deformation makes force sensing, contact detection, localization, and force estimation challenging, particularly when interactions occur at unknown arc-length locations along the robot. To address this problem, we propose a cascade learning-based framework (CLF) for CMs instrumented with a single distributed Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR) fiber embedded along one side of the robot. The OFDR sensor provides dense strain measurements along the manipulator backbone, capturing strain perturbations caused by external interactions. The proposed CLF first detects contact using a Gradient Boosting classifier and then estimates contact location and interaction force magnitude using a CNN--FiLM model that predicts a spatial force distribution along the manipulator. Experimental validation on a sensorized tendon-driven CM in an obstructed environment demonstrates that a single distributed OFDR fiber provides sufficient information to jointly infer contact occurrence, location, and force in continuum manipulators.

</details>


### [89] [Push, Press, Slide: Mode-Aware Planar Contact Manipulation via Reduced-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12399)
*Melih Özcan,Ozgur S. Oguz,Umut Orguner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于接触拓扑选择和降阶运动学建模的模式感知框架，用于单臂和双臂平面非抓取操作，通过物理直观的离散模型库抽象复杂的接触力学，实现快速、无需优化的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面非抓取操作（如推动和按压滑动）在机器人任务中至关重要，但由于混合接触力学、欠驱动和不对称摩擦限制等挑战，传统方法需要计算昂贵的迭代控制，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出模式感知框架，将复杂的力矩-速度极限表面力学抽象为离散的物理直观模型库，系统地将各种单臂和双臂接触拓扑映射到简单的非完整公式（如简化的按压滑动运动采用独轮车模型），通过降阶模型锚定轨迹生成，计算所需物体力矩并通过直接代数分配器分配可行的摩擦有界接触力。

Result: 在仿真中展示了快速、无需优化的方法在多样化单臂和双臂操作任务中的有效性，框架能够确保长时程可行性并整合机械臂运动学。

Conclusion: 通过接触拓扑选择和降阶运动学建模，提出的模式感知框架为平面非抓取操作提供了一种计算高效、物理直观的解决方案，避免了传统迭代控制的复杂性，在单臂和双臂操作中均表现出良好性能。

Abstract: Non-prehensile planar manipulation, including pushing and press-and-slide, is critical for diverse robotic tasks, but notoriously challenging due to hybrid contact mechanics, under-actuation, and asymmetric friction limits that traditionally necessitate computationally expensive iterative control. In this paper, we propose a mode-aware framework for planar manipulation with one or two robotic arms based on contact topology selection and reduced-order kinematic modeling. Our core insight is that complex wrench-twist limit surface mechanics can be abstracted into a discrete library of physically intuitive models. We systematically map various single-arm and bimanual contact topologies to simple non-holonomic formulations, e.g. unicycle for simplified press-and-slide motion. By anchoring trajectory generation to these reduced-order models, our framework computes the required object wrench and distributes feasible, friction-bounded contact forces via a direct algebraic allocator. We incorporate manipulator kinematics to ensure long-horizon feasibility and demonstrate our fast, optimization-free approach in simulation across diverse single-arm and bimanual manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Motion Imitation: Is Human Motion Data Alone Sufficient to Explain Gait Control and Biomechanics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12408)
*Xinyi Liu,Jangwhan Ahn,Edgar Lobaton,Jennie Si,He Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在基于强化学习的运动模仿学习框架中，添加足地交互测量作为奖励项如何影响人体步态运动学和动力学估计，发现仅准确复制运动学不能保证生物力学上合理的关节动力学，需要加入足地接触和接触力信息才能获得物理一致的步态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着运动模仿学习在人体生物力学和可穿戴机器人领域的兴趣增长，本研究旨在探索如何通过添加足地交互测量作为奖励项来改进人体步态运动学和动力学估计，解决传统仅关注运动学匹配的方法可能忽视物理一致性的问题。

Method: 采用基于强化学习的运动模仿学习框架，通过将足地接触和接触力信息作为额外的奖励项，与仅使用运动学信息的传统方法进行对比，评估这些附加约束对步态运动学和动力学估计的影响。

Result: 结果显示，仅准确复制前向运动学不能确保生物力学上合理的关节动力学。添加足地接触和接触力作为模仿学习奖励项后，前向行走模拟中预测的关节力矩与逆动力学计算的结果显著接近，表明动力学约束（特别是地面反作用力和压力中心信息）显著增强了内部和外部动力学的真实性。

Conclusion: 当模仿学习应用于生物力学和可穿戴机器人协同设计等人相关研究领域时，基于动力学的奖励塑造对于实现物理一致的步态表示是必要的，这揭示了纯运动学模仿学习方法在物理一致性方面的根本局限性。

Abstract: With the growing interest in motion imitation learning (IL) for human biomechanics and wearable robotics, this study investigates how additional foot-ground interaction measures, used as reward terms, affect human gait kinematics and kinetics estimation within a reinforcement learning-based IL framework. Results indicate that accurate reproduction of forward kinematics alone does not ensure biomechanically plausible joint kinetics. Adding foot-ground contacts and contact forces to the IL reward terms enables the prediction of joint moments in forward walking simulation, which are significantly closer to those computed by inverse dynamics. This finding highlights a fundamental limitation of motion-only IL approaches, which may prioritize kinematics matching over physical consistency. Incorporating kinetic constraints, particularly ground reaction force and center of pressure information, significantly enhances the realism of internal and external kinetics. These findings suggest that, when imitation learning is applied to human-related research domains such as biomechanics and wearable robot co-design, kinetics-based reward shaping is necessary to achieve physically consistent gait representations.

</details>


### [91] [Predictive and adaptive maps for long-term visual navigation in changing environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12460)
*Lucie Halodova,Eliska Dvorakova,Filip Majer,Tomas Vintr,Oscar Martinez Mozos,Feras Dayoub,Tomas Krajnik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较不同地图管理技术在变化环境中的长期视觉导航性能，发现建模环境周期性变化并预测特征可见性的策略优于不显式建模时间演化的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变化环境中实现可靠的长期视觉导航需要系统能够持续更新和优化特征地图以适应环境外观变化。地图管理技术需要能够选择对当前导航任务有用的特征、移除过时的特征、并从当前相机视图中添加新特征。

Method: 提出了多种地图管理策略，并在长期"教导-重复"导航中评估这些策略对机器人定位精度的影响。实验持续三个月，比较了是否显式建模环境变化时间演化的不同策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，那些建模环境外观周期性变化并预测特定时间和地点哪些特征将可见的策略，其性能优于不显式建模变化时间演化的策略。

Conclusion: 对于长期视觉导航，显式建模环境变化的周期性时间演化并预测特征可见性的地图管理策略更为有效，能够提供更好的定位精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we compare different map management techniques for long-term visual navigation in changing environments. In this scenario, the navigation system needs to continuously update and refine its feature map in order to adapt to the environment appearance change. To achieve reliable long-term navigation, the map management techniques have to (i) select features useful for the current navigation task, (ii) remove features that are obsolete, (iii) and add new features from the current camera view to the map. We propose several map management strategies and evaluate their performance with regard to the robot localisation accuracy in long-term teach-and-repeat navigation. Our experiments, performed over three months, indicate that strategies which model cyclic changes of the environment appearance and predict which features are going to be visible at a particular time and location, outperform strategies which do not explicitly model the temporal evolution of the changes.

</details>


### [92] [One-Step Flow Policy: Self-Distillation for Fast Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12480)
*Shaolong Li,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出One-Step Flow Policy (OFP)，一种无需预训练教师模型的自蒸馏框架，实现单步动作生成，解决生成流和扩散模型因迭代采样导致的高延迟问题，在机器人控制中实现100倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成流和扩散模型能提供连续多模态的动作分布，适合高精度机器人策略，但依赖迭代采样导致严重推理延迟，降低控制频率并损害时间敏感操作性能。

Method: OFP采用从头开始的自蒸馏框架，包含：1) 自一致性损失确保时间间隔内的连贯传输；2) 自引导正则化使预测向专家模式的高密度区域锐化；3) 热启动机制利用时间动作相关性最小化生成传输距离。

Result: 在56个多样化模拟操作任务中，单步OFP达到最先进结果，优于100步扩散和流策略，动作生成加速超过100倍。在RoboTwin 2.0的π₀.₅模型中集成OFP，单步OFP超越原始10步策略。

Conclusion: OFP为高精度、低延迟机器人控制提供了实用、可扩展的解决方案，通过单步生成实现高效动作预测，显著降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Generative flow and diffusion models provide the continuous, multimodal action distributions needed for high-precision robotic policies. However, their reliance on iterative sampling introduces severe inference latency, degrading control frequency and harming performance in time-sensitive manipulation. To address this problem, we propose the One-Step Flow Policy (OFP), a from-scratch self-distillation framework for high-fidelity, single-step action generation without a pre-trained teacher. OFP unifies a self-consistency loss to enforce coherent transport across time intervals, and a self-guided regularization to sharpen predictions toward high-density expert modes. In addition, a warm-start mechanism leverages temporal action correlations to minimize the generative transport distance. Evaluations across 56 diverse simulated manipulation tasks demonstrate that a one-step OFP achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming 100-step diffusion and flow policies while accelerating action generation by over $100\times$. We further integrate OFP into the $π_{0.5}$ model on RoboTwin 2.0, where one-step OFP surpasses the original 10-step policy. These results establish OFP as a practical, scalable solution for highly accurate and low-latency robot control.

</details>


### [93] [COAD: Constant-Time Planning for Continuous Goal Manipulation with Compressed Library and Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12488)
*Adil Shiyas,Zhuoyun Zhong,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COAD框架通过将连续任务空间离散化为有限任务覆盖区域，仅离线求解代表性根问题构建压缩运动库，实现连续目标参数化任务空间的常数时间规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作任务中，机器人需要反复解决运动规划问题，这些问题主要差异在于目标物体及其相关障碍物的位置，而周围工作空间保持固定。现有方法虽然利用经验和离线计算加速重复规划查询，但缺乏连续任务空间的覆盖保证，且需要存储大量解决方案库。

Method: COAD将连续任务空间离散化为有限数量的任务覆盖区域。不针对每个区域离线规划并存储解决方案，而是仅求解代表性根问题构建压缩库。其他问题通过从这些根解决方案快速适应处理。查询时，系统在常数时间内检索根运动，并使用轻量级适应模块（如线性插值、动态运动基元或简单轨迹优化）将其适应到期望目标。

Result: 在多种机械臂和环境的仿真及真实世界评估中，COAD实现了运动库的显著压缩，同时保持高成功率，查询时间达到亚毫秒级别，在效率和路径质量方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: COAD框架通过任务空间离散化和基于根解决方案的快速适应，提供了连续目标参数化任务空间的常数时间规划能力，实现了运动库的高效压缩和快速查询，为重复性机器人操作任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In many robotic manipulation tasks, the robot repeatedly solves motion-planning problems that differ mainly in the location of the goal object and its associated obstacle, while the surrounding workspace remains fixed. Prior works have shown that leveraging experience and offline computation can accelerate repeated planning queries, but they lack guarantees of covering the continuous task space and require storing large libraries of solutions. In this work, we present COAD, a framework that provides constant-time planning over a continuous goal-parameterized task space. COAD discretizes the continuous task space into finitely many Task Coverage Regions. Instead of planning and storing solutions for every region offline, it constructs a compressed library by only solving representative root problems. Other problems are handled through fast adaptation from these root solutions. At query time, the system retrieves a root motion in constant time and adapts it to the desired goal using lightweight adaptation modules such as linear interpolation, Dynamic Movement Primitives, or simple trajectory optimization. We evaluate the framework on various manipulators and environments in simulation and the real world, showing that COAD achieves substantial compression of the motion library while maintaining high success rates and sub-millisecond-level queries, outperforming baseline methods in both efficiency and path quality. The source code is available at https://github.com/elpis-lab/CoAd.

</details>


### [94] [Robots that redesign themselves through kinematic self-destruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12505)
*Chen Yu,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人通过自毁机制在生命周期中主动参与自身设计，从随机组装的躯体开始，利用本体感觉反馈动态"雕刻"自身，移除阻碍运动的冗余关节，实现更有效的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人都是预先设计好的，缺乏在部署后根据环境需求主动调整自身形态的能力。本研究旨在探索机器人能否在生命周期中主动参与自身设计，通过适应性调整身体结构来优化性能。

Method: 采用单一自回归序列模型作为通用控制器，在仿真中学习何时以及如何通过自毁机制简化机器人身体结构。机器人从随机组装的躯体开始，利用本体感觉反馈识别阻碍运动的冗余关节，然后通过剧烈摆动使其在关节处断裂脱落。

Result: 优化后的策略成功迁移到现实世界，并能泛化到未见过的运动学树结构。通过自毁机制简化的机器人比随机移除关节或无法移除关节的同等策略产生更有效的前向运动。

Conclusion: 自设计机器人在某些情况下可能比预先设计的机器人更成功，运动学自毁虽然是一种还原性和不可逆的过程，但可能为广泛机器人提供一种通用的适应性策略。

Abstract: Every robot built to date was predesigned by an external process, prior to deployment. Here we show a robot that actively participates in its own design during its lifetime. Starting from a randomly assembled body, and using only proprioceptive feedback, the robot dynamically ``sculpts'' itself into a new design through kinematic self-destruction: identifying redundant links within its body that inhibit its locomotion, and then thrashing those links against the surface until they break at the joint and fall off the body. It does so using a single autoregressive sequence model, a universal controller that learns in simulation when and how to simplify a robot's body through self-destruction and then adaptively controls the reduced morphology. The optimized policy successfully transfers to reality and generalizes to previously unseen kinematic trees, generating forward locomotion that is more effective than otherwise equivalent policies that randomly remove links or cannot remove any. This suggests that self-designing robots may be more successful than predesigned robots in some cases, and that kinematic self-destruction, though reductive and irreversible, could provide a general adaptive strategy for a wide range of robots.

</details>


### [95] [Red-Teaming Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Prompt Generation for Robust Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12510)
*Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Sophie Hsu,Varun Bhatt,Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Bryon Tjanaka,Minjune Hwang,Akanksha Saran,Daniel Seita,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q-DIG使用质量多样性(QD)技术和视觉语言模型(VLM)生成多样化的对抗性指令，以识别VLA模型的漏洞并提高其鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人系统中具有巨大潜力，但其性能对语言指令的措辞高度敏感，且难以预测失败情况，需要提高对不同措辞的鲁棒性

Method: Q-DIG整合质量多样性(QD)技术和视觉语言模型(VLM)，通过可扩展地识别多样化的自然语言任务描述来发现失败模式，同时保持任务相关性

Result: 在多个模拟基准测试中，Q-DIG相比基线方法发现了更多样化和有意义的失败模式；基于生成指令的微调提高了任务成功率；用户研究显示Q-DIG生成的提示更自然、更像人类；真实世界评估结果与模拟一致

Conclusion: Q-DIG是识别VLA模型漏洞和提高机器人系统鲁棒性的有前景方法，通过生成对抗性指令进行微调可以提升对未见指令的泛化能力

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significant potential to enable general-purpose robotic systems for a range of vision-language tasks. However, the performance of VLA-based robots is highly sensitive to the precise wording of language instructions, and it remains difficult to predict when such robots will fail. To improve the robustness of VLAs to different wordings, we present Q-DIG (Quality Diversity for Diverse Instruction Generation), which performs red-teaming by scalably identifying diverse natural language task descriptions that induce failures while remaining task-relevant. Q-DIG integrates Quality Diversity (QD) techniques with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate a broad spectrum of adversarial instructions that expose meaningful vulnerabilities in VLA behavior. Our results across multiple simulation benchmarks show that Q-DIG finds more diverse and meaningful failure modes compared to baseline methods, and that fine-tuning VLAs on the generated instructions improves task success rates. Furthermore, results from a user study highlight that Q-DIG generates prompts judged to be more natural and human-like than those from baselines. Finally, real-world evaluations of Q-DIG prompts show results consistent with simulation, and fine-tuning VLAs on the generated prompts further success rates on unseen instructions. Together, these findings suggest that Q-DIG is a promising approach for identifying vulnerabilities and improving the robustness of VLA-based robots. Our anonymous project website is at qdigvla.github.io.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Dense Futures: World Models as Structured Planners for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12553)
*Minghao Jin,Mozheng Liao,Mingfei Han,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StructVLA：一种基于结构化帧预测的视觉-语言-动作架构，通过稀疏的物理意义结构化帧替代密集视觉预测，解决长时程规划漂移问题，提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型方法存在两个主要问题：1）密集未来预测引入视觉冗余并累积误差，导致长时程规划漂移；2）稀疏方法通常使用高级语义子任务或隐式潜在状态表示，缺乏明确的运动学基础，导致规划与低级执行对齐不足。

Method: 提出StructVLA架构，将生成式世界模型重构为显式结构化规划器。通过预测稀疏的物理意义结构化帧（基于内在运动学线索如夹爪状态转换和运动学转折点），捕捉与任务进展紧密对齐的时空里程碑。采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段训练世界模型预测结构化帧，第二阶段优化模型将结构化前瞻映射到低级动作。

Result: 在SimperEnv-WidowX上达到75.0%的平均成功率，在LIBERO上达到94.8%的平均成功率。真实世界部署进一步证明了在基础拾放任务和复杂长时程任务上的可靠任务完成和鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: StructVLA通过结构化帧预测有效解决了视觉冗余和规划漂移问题，提供了明确的物理指导，桥接了视觉规划与运动控制，在模拟和真实环境中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Recent world-model-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures have improved robotic manipulation through predictive visual foresight. However, dense future prediction introduces visual redundancy and accumulates errors, causing long-horizon plan drift. Meanwhile, recent sparse methods typically represent visual foresight using high-level semantic subtasks or implicit latent states. These representations often lack explicit kinematic grounding, weakening the alignment between planning and low-level execution. To address this, we propose StructVLA, which reformulates a generative world model into an explicit structured planner for reliable control. Instead of dense rollouts or semantic goals, StructVLA predicts sparse, physically meaningful structured frames. Derived from intrinsic kinematic cues (e.g., gripper transitions and kinematic turning points), these frames capture spatiotemporal milestones closely aligned with task progress. We implement this approach through a two-stage training paradigm with a unified discrete token vocabulary: the world model is first trained to predict structured frames and subsequently optimized to map the structured foresight into low-level actions. This approach provides clear physical guidance and bridges visual planning and motion control. In our experiments, StructVLA achieves strong average success rates of 75.0% on SimplerEnv-WidowX and 94.8% on LIBERO. Real-world deployments further demonstrate reliable task completion and robust generalization across both basic pick-and-place and complex long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [97] [From Woofs to Words: Towards Intelligent Robotic Guide Dogs with Verbal Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12574)
*Yohei Hayamizu,David DeFazio,Hrudayangam Mehta,Zainab Altaweel,Jacqueline Choe,Chao Lin,Jake Juettner,Furui Xiao,Jeremy Blackburn,Shiqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发用于机器人导盲犬的新型对话系统，利用LLM将导航计划和场景信息语言化，实现人机团队协作决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助机器人领域中的机器人导盲犬需要语言能力，但现有对话系统无法满足动态环境中的语言接地和提升人类操作者空间感知的需求

Method: 开发基于LLM的对话系统，将导航计划和场景信息语言化，支持人机协作决策；通过人类研究评估不同语言化策略，通过仿真研究评估导航任务的效率和准确性

Result: 通过实验验证了不同语言化策略的效果，并评估了导航任务的效率和准确性

Conclusion: 提出的对话系统能够有效解决机器人导盲犬在动态环境中的语言接地和空间感知问题，实现人机团队的协作决策

Abstract: Assistive robotics is an important subarea of robotics that focuses on the well-being of people with disabilities. A robotic guide dog is an assistive quadruped robot that helps visually impaired people in obstacle avoidance and navigation. Enabling language capabilities for robotic guide dogs goes beyond naively adding an existing dialog system onto a mobile robot. The novel challenges include grounding language in the dynamically changing environment and improving spatial awareness for the human handler. To address those challenges, we develop a novel dialog system for robotic guide dogs that uses LLMs to verbalize both navigational plans and scenes. The goal is to enable verbal communication for collaborative decision-making within the handler-robot team. In experiments, we conducted a human study to evaluate different verbalization strategies and a simulation study to assess the efficiency and accuracy in navigation tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Skill-informed Data-driven Haptic Nudges for High-dimensional Human Motor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12583)
*Ankur Kamboj,Rajiv Ranganathan,Xiaobo Tan,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数据驱动技能信息的框架，为高维新颖运动学习任务设计最优触觉提示反馈，通过IOHMM建模人类运动学习随机动态，将反馈设计转化为POMDP问题，实验证明POMDP策略显著加速学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高维新颖运动学习任务设计有效的触觉提示反馈面临挑战，传统启发式方法缺乏理论基础，需要数据驱动的优化框架来加速学习过程并引导学习者发现高效的运动表征。

Method: 1. 使用输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型(IOHMM)建模人类运动学习的随机动态，将潜在技能演化与可观测运动学输出解耦；2. 基于预测模型将触觉提示反馈设计问题形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)；3. 推导最小化长期性能成本的最优提示策略，引导学习者进入技能空间的稳健区域。

Result: 30人参与的手部外骨骼任务实验表明：接受POMDP策略反馈的参与者任务表现显著加速，优于启发式反馈组和无反馈组；协同性分析显示POMDP组更快发现高效的低维运动表征。

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动技能信息框架成功将触觉提示反馈设计转化为POMDP优化问题，能够加速高维运动学习并促进高效运动表征的发现，为智能辅助系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a data-driven skill-informed framework to design optimal haptic nudge feedback for high-dimensional novel motor learning tasks. We first model the stochastic dynamics of human motor learning using an Input-Output Hidden Markov Model (IOHMM), which explicitly decouples latent skill evolution from observable kinematic emissions. Leveraging this predictive model, we formulate the haptic nudge feedback design problem as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). This allows us to derive an optimal nudging policy that minimizes long-term performance cost, implicitly guiding the learner toward robust regions of the skill space. We validated our approach through a human-subject study ($N=30$) using a high-dimensional hand-exoskeleton task. Results demonstrate that participants trained with the POMDP-derived policy exhibited significantly accelerated task performance compared to groups receiving heuristic-based feedback or no feedback. Furthermore, synergy analysis revealed that the POMDP group discovered efficient low-dimensional motor representations more rapidly.

</details>


### [99] [CarPLAN: Context-Adaptive and Robust Planning with Dynamic Scene Awareness for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12607)
*Junyong Yun,Jungho Kim,ByungHyun Lee,Dongyoung Lee,Sehwan Choi,Seunghyeop Nam,Kichun Jo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CarPLAN是一个基于模仿学习的运动规划框架，通过位移感知预测编码增强空间感知，结合上下文自适应多专家解码器实现动态环境中的自适应规划，在nuPlan和Waymax基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的模仿学习规划需要捕捉真实驾驶数据的复杂上下文，并实现上下文自适应决策，而不仅仅是模仿专家轨迹。现有方法在复杂交通场景中的上下文理解和自适应规划能力不足。

Method: 提出CarPLAN框架：1) 位移感知预测编码(DPE)通过预测AV与周围场景元素之间的未来位移向量增强空间感知；2) 上下文自适应多专家解码器(CMD)基于MoE框架，根据场景结构动态选择最合适的专家解码器；3) 结合模仿损失和位移预测误差的增强损失项。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，CarPLAN在所有闭环仿真指标上达到最先进性能，在Test14-Hard等挑战性场景中表现稳健。在Waymax基准测试上的额外实验进一步证明了其在不同基准设置下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CarPLAN通过显式增强驾驶上下文理解和自适应规划能力，显著提升了模仿学习在自动驾驶运动规划中的性能，特别是在复杂驾驶条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) is widely used for motion planning in autonomous driving due to its data efficiency and access to real-world driving data. For safe and robust real-world driving, IL-based planning requires capturing the complex driving contexts inherent in real-world data and enabling context-adaptive decision-making, rather than relying solely on expert trajectory imitation. In this paper, we propose CarPLAN, a novel IL-based motion planning framework that explicitly enhances driving context understanding and enables adaptive planning across diverse traffic scenarios. Our contributions are twofold: We introduce Displacement-Aware Predictive Encoding (DPE) to improve the model's spatial awareness by predicting future displacement vectors between the Autonomous Vehicle (AV) and surrounding scene elements. This allows the planner to account for relational spacing when generating trajectories. In addition to the standard imitation loss, we incorporate an augmented loss term that captures displacement prediction errors, ensuring planning decisions consider relative distances from other agents. To improve the model's ability to handle diverse driving contexts, we propose Context-Adaptive Multi-Expert Decoder (CMD), which leverages the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework. CMD dynamically selects the most suitable expert decoders based on scene structure at each Transformer layer, enabling adaptive and context-aware planning in dynamic environments. We evaluate CarPLAN on the nuPlan benchmark and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all closed-loop simulation metrics. In particular, CarPLAN exhibits robust performance on challenging scenarios such as Test14-Hard, validating its effectiveness in complex driving conditions. Additional experiments on the Waymax benchmark further demonstrate its generalization capability across different benchmark settings.

</details>


### [100] [Autonomous Integration and Improvement of Robotic Assembly using Skill Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12649)
*Peiqi Yu,Philip Huang,Chaitanya Chawla,Guanya Shi,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于技能图表示的机器人装配系统自主集成与持续改进框架，通过语义级规划和闭环学习实现自适应可重用装配系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人装配系统需要大量人工工程工作来集成新任务、适应新环境和随时间改进性能，缺乏自适应和可重用性

Method: 使用技能图表示法，将机器人能力组织为基于动词的技能，明确连接语义描述（动词和名词）与可执行策略、前置条件、后置条件和评估器，支持语义级规划和系统化数据收集

Result: 技能图框架统一了系统配置、执行、评估和学习，为自适应可重用机器人装配系统提供了可扩展路径，代码已开源

Conclusion: 技能图表示能够实现机器人装配系统的快速集成和持续改进，通过单一表示统一多个系统功能，推动自适应可重用装配系统的发展

Abstract: Robotic assembly systems traditionally require substantial manual engineering effort to integrate new tasks, adapt to new environments, and improve performance over time. This paper presents a framework for autonomous integration and continuous improvement of robotic assembly systems based on Skill Graph representations. A Skill Graph organizes robot capabilities as verb-based skills, explicitly linking semantic descriptions (verbs and nouns) with executable policies, pre-conditions, post-conditions, and evaluators. We show how Skill Graphs enable rapid system integration by supporting semantic-level planning over skills, while simultaneously grounding execution through well-defined interfaces to robot controllers and perception modules. After initial deployment, the same Skill Graph structure supports systematic data collection and closed-loop performance improvement, enabling iterative refinement of skills and their composition. We demonstrate how this approach unifies system configuration, execution, evaluation, and learning within a single representation, providing a scalable pathway toward adaptive and reusable robotic assembly systems. The code is at https://github.com/intelligent-control-lab/AIDF.

</details>


### [101] [TacVLA: Contact-Aware Tactile Fusion for Robust Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12665)
*Kaidi Zhang,Heng Zhang,Zhengtong Xu,Zhiyuan Zhang,Md Rakibul Islam Prince,Xiang Li,Xiaojing Han,Yuhao Zhou,Arash Ajoudani,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacVLA：通过触觉模态增强的视觉-语言-动作模型，提升机器人精细操作能力，在视觉遮挡、接触丰富任务中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖视觉和语言模态，在视觉遮挡、精细操作和物理接触任务中表现不佳，需要引入触觉感知来增强精细操作能力

Method: 提出TacVLA模型，在基于transformer的策略中融入触觉模态，引入接触感知门控机制，仅在检测到接触时激活触觉token，实现自适应多模态融合

Result: 在约束锁定拆卸、箱内拾取和鲁棒性评估中优于基线，拆卸任务成功率平均提升20%，箱内拾取提升60%，视觉遮挡场景性能提升2.1倍

Conclusion: 触觉模态的融入显著增强了VLA模型在精细操作和接触丰富任务中的性能，接触感知门控机制有效避免了无关触觉干扰，实现了更鲁棒的跨模态融合

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated significant advantages in robotic manipulation. However, their reliance on vision and language often leads to suboptimal performance in tasks involving visual occlusion, fine-grained manipulation, and physical contact. To address these challenges, we propose TacVLA, a fine-tuned VLA model by incorporating tactile modalities into the transformer-based policy to enhance fine-grained manipulation capabilities. Specifically, we introduce a contact-aware gating mechanism that selectively activates tactile tokens only when contact is detected, enabling adaptive multimodal fusion while avoiding irrelevant tactile interference. The fused visual, language, and tactile tokens are jointly processed within the transformer architecture to strengthen cross-modal grounding during contact-rich interaction. Extensive experiments on constraint-locked disassembly, in-box picking and robustness evaluations demonstrate that our model outperforms baselines, improving the performance by averaging 20% success rate in disassembly, 60% in in-box picking and 2.1x improvement in scenarios with visual occlusion. Videos are available at https://sites.google.com/view/tacvla and code will be released.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Athletic Humanoid Tennis Skills from Imperfect Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12686)
*Zhikai Zhang,Haofei Lu,Yunrui Lian,Ziqing Chen,Yun Liu,Chenghuai Lin,Han Xue,Zicheng Zeng,Zekun Qi,Shaolin Zheng,Qing Luan,Jingbo Wang,Junliang Xing,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LATENT系统通过学习不完美的人类网球动作数据，让仿人机器人掌握动态的网球技能，实现稳定的多拍对打


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动员在网球比赛中展现出高度动态和多样化的技能，但将这些行为复现到仿人机器人上很困难，主要因为缺乏完美的仿人动作数据或网球场景中的人类运动学数据作为参考

Method: 提出LATENT系统，从不完美的人类网球动作片段中学习仿人网球技能。这些不完美数据只包含捕捉网球基本技能的运动片段，而非完整精确的网球比赛动作序列。通过校正和组合这些数据，学习仿人策略，使其能在各种条件下稳定击打来球并回击到目标位置，同时保持自然的运动风格。还提出了一系列鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移设计

Result: 方法在真实世界中取得了令人惊讶的结果，能够在Unitree G1仿人机器人上稳定维持与人类玩家的多拍对打

Conclusion: LATENT系统证明，即使使用不完美的人类运动数据，通过适当的校正和组合，也能让仿人机器人学习到动态的网球技能，实现与人类玩家的稳定对打，为机器人学习复杂运动技能提供了新思路

Abstract: Human athletes demonstrate versatile and highly-dynamic tennis skills to successfully conduct competitive rallies with a high-speed tennis ball. However, reproducing such behaviors on humanoid robots is difficult, partially due to the lack of perfect humanoid action data or human kinematic motion data in tennis scenarios as reference. In this work, we propose LATENT, a system that Learns Athletic humanoid TEnnis skills from imperfect human motioN daTa. The imperfect human motion data consist only of motion fragments that capture the primitive skills used when playing tennis rather than precise and complete human-tennis motion sequences from real-world tennis matches, thereby significantly reducing the difficulty of data collection. Our key insight is that, despite being imperfect, such quasi-realistic data still provide priors about human primitive skills in tennis scenarios. With further correction and composition, we learn a humanoid policy that can consistently strike incoming balls under a wide range of conditions and return them to target locations, while preserving natural motion styles. We also propose a series of designs for robust sim-to-real transfer and deploy our policy on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot. Our method achieves surprising results in the real world and can stably sustain multi-shot rallies with human players. Project page: https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/

</details>


### [103] [Altered Thoughts, Altered Actions: Probing Chain-of-Thought Vulnerabilities in VLA Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12717)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现VLA模型的内部推理轨迹存在安全漏洞：仅通过替换推理文本中的物体名称就能显著降低机器人任务成功率，而更复杂的攻击（如LLM生成错误计划）影响有限，表明动作解码器主要依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型广泛采用链式思维推理，在解码运动命令前先生成自然语言计划。这种推理模块和动作解码器之间的内部文本通道从未受到对抗性审查。研究旨在探究：动作解码器实际依赖推理轨迹的哪些属性？仅针对性破坏推理轨迹（保持所有输入完整）能否降低机器人的物理任务性能？

Method: 设计了七种文本破坏方法，分为三个攻击层级：盲噪声、机械语义和LLM自适应攻击。在40个LIBERO桌面操作任务上对最先进的推理VLA模型进行测试。使用非推理VLA模型进行跨架构对照，验证漏洞是否特定于推理增强模型。

Result: 结果显示显著不对称性：替换推理轨迹中的物体名称使总体成功率降低8.3个百分点，在目标条件任务上达到-19.3pp，个别任务甚至-45pp；而句子重排、空间方向反转、令牌噪声甚至70B参数LLM生成的看似合理但错误的计划影响可忽略（在±4pp内）。这表明动作解码器依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量或顺序结构。LLM攻击者表现不如简单的机械物体名称替换，因为保持合理性无意中保留了解码器所需的实体接地结构。

Conclusion: 内部推理轨迹是推理增强VLA模型的独特且隐蔽的威胁向量，对输入验证防御不可见。动作解码器主要依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量，这一发现揭示了VLA模型安全性的关键漏洞，并为未来防御机制设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, generating a natural-language plan before decoding motor commands. This internal text channel between the reasoning module and the action decoder has received no adversarial scrutiny. We ask: which properties of this intermediate plan does the action decoder actually rely on, and can targeted corruption of the reasoning trace alone -- with all inputs left intact -- degrade a robot's physical task performance? We design a taxonomy of seven text corruptions organized into three attacker tiers (blind noise, mechanical-semantic, and LLM-adaptive) and apply them to a state-of-the-art reasoning VLA across 40 LIBERO tabletop manipulation tasks. Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: substituting object names in the reasoning trace reduces overall success rate by 8.3~percentage points (pp) -- reaching $-$19.3~pp on goal-conditioned tasks and $-$45~pp on individual tasks -- whereas sentence reordering, spatial-direction reversal, token noise, and even a 70B-parameter LLM crafting plausible-but-wrong plans all have negligible impact (within $\pm$4~pp). This asymmetry indicates that the action decoder depends on entity-reference integrity rather than reasoning quality or sequential structure. Notably, a sophisticated LLM-based attacker underperforms simple mechanical object-name substitution, because preserving plausibility inadvertently retains the entity-grounding structure the decoder needs. A cross-architecture control using a non-reasoning VLA confirms the vulnerability is exclusive to reasoning-augmented models, while instruction-level attacks degrade both architectures -- establishing that the internal reasoning trace is a distinct and stealthy threat vector invisible to input-validation defenses.

</details>


### [104] [Conflict Mitigation in Shared Environments using Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12736)
*Lukas Heuer,Yufei Zhu,Luigi Palmieri,Andrey Rudenko,Anna Mannucci,Sven Koenig,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FA-MAPF框架通过集成不可控智能体的学习运动模式来提升多机器人系统在动态环境中的路径规划质量，减少冲突达55%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态不可控智能体共存的环境中，大规模机器人车队部署面临挑战，现有研究主要关注延迟情况下的MAPF解决方案完整性，但缺乏利用额外环境信息提升解决方案质量的研究

Method: 提出Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding (FA-MAPF)框架，将学习到的不可控智能体运动模式集成到集中式MAPF算法中

Result: 在多样化基准地图和真实世界地图上的评估显示，FA-MAPF相比最先进基线方法能持续减少与不可控智能体的冲突，最高达55%，且不牺牲任务效率

Conclusion: FA-MAPF通过整合不可控智能体的运动模式信息，有效提升了多机器人系统在动态共享环境中的路径规划性能

Abstract: Deploying multi-robot systems in environments shared with dynamic and uncontrollable agents presents significant challenges, especially for large robot fleets. In such environments, individual robot operations can be delayed due to unforeseen conflicts with uncontrollable agents. While existing research primarily focuses on preserving the completeness of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) solutions considering delays, there is limited emphasis on utilizing additional environmental information to enhance solution quality in the presence of other dynamic agents. To this end, we propose Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding (FA-MAPF), a novel framework that integrates learned motion patterns of uncontrollable agents into centralized MAPF algorithms. Our evaluation, conducted on a diverse set of benchmark maps with simulated uncontrollable agents and on a real-world map with recorded human trajectories, demonstrates the effectiveness of FA-MAPF compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The experimental results show that FA-MAPF can consistently reduce conflicts with uncontrollable agents, up to 55%, without compromising task efficiency.

</details>


### [105] [Easy-IIL: Reducing Human Operational Burden in Interactive Imitation Learning via Assistant Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12769)
*Chengjie Zhang,Chao Tang,Wenlong Dong,Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Easy-IIL框架利用现成的基于模型的模仿学习方法作为辅助专家，替代人类进行大部分数据收集，显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持与主流交互式模仿学习基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式模仿学习需要大量人类参与离线演示和在线交互，现有工作主要关注减少被动监控而非主动操作的人力。基于模型的模仿方法在低数据量时表现与端到端方法相当但需要更少演示，但数据增加时通常被端到端方法超越。作者希望利用这一洞察减少人类主动操作负担。

Method: 提出Easy-IIL框架：1) 人类专家仅提供单个演示初始化辅助专家；2) 使用现成的基于模型模仿方法作为辅助专家替代人类进行大部分数据收集；3) 人类仅在任务接近失败的关键状态进行干预；4) 通过保持离线和在线数据质量来维持IIL性能。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，Easy-IIL显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持与主流IIL基线相当的性能。用户研究进一步证实Easy-IIL减少了人类专家的主观工作负荷。

Conclusion: Easy-IIL通过利用基于模型的模仿方法作为辅助专家，有效减少了交互式模仿学习中人类主动操作的负担，在保持性能的同时提高了学习效率。

Abstract: Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) typically relies on extensive human involvement for both offline demonstration and online interaction. Prior work primarily focuses on reducing human effort in passive monitoring rather than active operation. Interestingly, structured model-based imitation approaches achieve comparable performance with significantly fewer demonstrations than end-to-end imitation learning policies in the low-data regime. However, these methods are typically surpassed by end-to-end policies as the data increases. Leveraging this insight, we propose Easy-IIL, a framework that utilizes off-the-shelf model-based imitation methods as an assistant expert to replace active human operation for the majority of data collection. The human expert only provides a single demonstration to initialize the assistant expert and intervenes in critical states where the task is approaching failure. Furthermore, Easy-IIL can maintain IIL performance by preserving both offline and online data quality. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Easy-IIL significantly reduces human operational burden while maintaining performance comparable to mainstream IIL baselines. User studies further confirm that Easy-IIL reduces subjective workload on the human expert. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/easy-iil

</details>


### [106] [Motion-Specific Battery Health Assessment for Quadrotors Using High-Fidelity Battery Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12791)
*Joonhee Kim,Sanghyun Park,Donghyeong Kim,Eunseon Choi,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个端到端框架，用于评估四旋翼飞行器运动对电池健康的影响，通过实际飞行电流测量和校准的高保真电池模型，分析不同飞行动作如何导致不同的电池退化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能量感知规划将电池视为简单的能量储存器，忽略了飞行运动引起的动态电流负载会加速电池退化。需要理解不同飞行动作如何影响电池健康，以实现平衡效率和电池退化的运动感知电池管理。

Method: 1) 设计宽范围电流传感模块捕获实际飞行中的运动特定电流剖面；2) 使用参考性能测试和基于退化耦合电化学模型的元启发式方法校准高保真电池模型；3) 在已校准模型中模拟测量的飞行负载，系统分析不同飞行动作如何转化为退化模式（锂库存损失、活性材料损失和内部副反应）。

Result: 即使两个飞行剖面消耗相同的平均能量，它们的瞬态负载结构也会驱动不同的退化途径。研究强调了需要运动感知的电池管理，以平衡效率和电池退化。

Conclusion: 提出了一个端到端框架，用于四旋翼飞行器的运动感知电池健康评估。通过结合实际飞行电流测量和校准的高保真电池模型，能够系统分析不同飞行动作如何转化为特定的电池退化模式，为实现平衡效率和电池寿命的运动感知电池管理提供了基础。

Abstract: Quadrotor endurance is ultimately limited by battery behavior, yet most energy aware planning treats the battery as a simple energy reservoir and overlooks how flight motions induce dynamic current loads that accelerate battery degradation. This work presents an end to end framework for motion aware battery health assessment in quadrotors. We first design a wide range current sensing module to capture motion specific current profiles during real flights, preserving transient features. In parallel, a high fidelity battery model is calibrated using reference performance tests and a metaheuristic based on a degradation coupled electrochemical model.By simulating measured flight loads in the calibrated model, we systematically resolve how different flight motions translate into degradation modes loss of lithium inventory and loss of active material as well as internal side reactions. The results demonstrate that even when two flight profiles consume the same average energy, their transient load structures can drive different degradation pathways, emphasizing the need for motion-aware battery management that balances efficiency with battery degradation.

</details>


### [107] [FLUX: Accelerating Cross-Embodiment Generative Navigation Policies via Rectified Flow and Static-to-Dynamic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12806)
*Zeying Gong,Yangyi Zhong,Yiyi Ding,Tianshuai Hu,Guoyang Zhao,Lingdong Kong,Rong Li,Jiadi You,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynBench动态导航基准和FLUX统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流实现高效推理，在静态到动态课程学习中实现SOTA性能，并展示零样本迁移能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主导航评估存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的评估框架来覆盖从静态目标到达到动态社交穿越的完整技能谱系

Method: 1) 引入DynBench动态导航基准，结合物理有效的群体仿真；2) 提出FLUX基于流的统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流将迭代去噪替换为直线轨迹；3) 采用静态到动态课程学习，先建立几何先验，再通过强化学习在动态社交环境中精炼

Result: FLUX相比先前基于流的方法提升单步推理效率47%，相比基于扩散的方法提升29%；在六个基本导航任务上实现SOTA性能；在轮式、四足和人形平台上实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，无需微调

Conclusion: DynBench提供了全面的导航评估框架，FLUX通过流线性化和课程学习实现了高效、鲁棒的统一导航策略，具备跨平台零样本迁移能力，为自主导航系统提供了新的基准和方法

Abstract: Autonomous navigation requires a broad spectrum of skills, from static goal-reaching to dynamic social traversal, yet evaluation remains fragmented across disparate protocols. We introduce DynBench, a dynamic navigation benchmark featuring physically valid crowd simulation. Combined with existing static protocols, it supports comprehensive evaluation across six fundamental navigation tasks. Within this framework, we propose FLUX, the first flow-based unified navigation policy. By linearizing probability flow, FLUX replaces iterative denoising with straight-line trajectories, improving per-step inference efficiency by 47% over prior flow-based methods and 29% over diffusion-based ones. Following a static-to-dynamic curriculum, FLUX initially establishes geometric priors and is subsequently refined through reinforcement learning in dynamic social environments. This regime not only strengthens socially-aware navigation but also enhances static task robustness by capturing recovery behaviors through stochastic action distributions. FLUX achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks and demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wheeled, quadrupedal, and humanoid platforms without any fine-tuning.

</details>


### [108] [Reinforcement Learning for Elliptical Cylinder Motion Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12807)
*Pawel Marczewski,Paulina Superczynska,Jakub Bernat,Szymon Szczesny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究椭圆圆柱体在有限扭矩输入下的控制问题，使用强化学习解决，并与基于能量整形的摆起控制器+LQR稳定器的经典方法进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受限于输入扭矩的设备控制问题，特别是受无缆磁控设备启发的椭圆圆柱体运动控制，这类问题由于距离限制只能使用有限输入扭矩，具有挑战性和非平凡解。

Method: 采用强化学习方法解决椭圆圆柱体有限扭矩控制问题，同时设计经典基准控制器：由能量整形摆起控制律和局部LQR稳定器组成的两阶段控制器。摆起控制器增加系统机械能量使状态接近目标平衡点，然后对非线性模型线性化得到LQR控制器，在输入有界条件下调节角度和角速度状态。

Result: 研究表明学习控制是可行的，但对于质量增加或长短轴比显著不等的椭圆，某些情况如向上位置稳定或半周转非常困难。摆起+LQR策略为欠驱动系统提供了强大且可解释的参考基准。

Conclusion: 椭圆圆柱体在有限扭矩下的控制问题可以通过强化学习解决，但某些特定情况仍具挑战性。经典的两阶段控制器为欠驱动系统提供了有效的基准比较方法。

Abstract: The control of devices with limited input always bring attention to solve by research due to its difficulty and non-trival solution. For instance, the inverted pendulum is benchmarking problem in control theory and machine learning. In this work, we are focused on the elliptical cylinder and its motion under limited torque. The inspiration of the problem is from untethered magnetic devices, which due to distance have to operate with limited input torque. In this work, the main goal is to define the control problem of elliptic cylinder with limited input torque and solve it by Reinforcement Learning. As a classical baseline, we evaluate a two-stage controller composed of an energy-shaping swing-up law and a local Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) stabilizer around the target equilibrium. The swing-up controller increases the system's mechanical energy to drive the state toward a neighborhood of the desired equilibrium, a linearization of the nonlinear model yields an LQR that regulates the angle and angular-rate states to the target orientation with bounded input. This swing-up + LQR policy is a strong, interpretable reference for underactuated system and serves a point of comparison to the learned policy under identical limits and parameters. The solution shows that the learning is possible however, the different cases like stabilization in upward position or rotating of half turn are very difficult for increasing mass or ellipses with a strongly unequal perimeter ratio.

</details>


### [109] [SmoothTurn: Learning to Turn Smoothly for Agile Navigation with Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12842)
*Zunzhi You,Haolan Guo,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothTurn：一种基于学习的控制框架，用于四足机器人在高速奔跑时实现平滑转向，以完成敏捷的顺序局部导航任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目标导航方法在需要连续改变方向时无法预判即将到来的机动动作或保持动量，限制了四足机器人在消防救援、工业巡检等实际应用中的敏捷性潜力。

Method: 将任务重新表述为顺序局部导航，采用新颖的顺序目标到达奖励函数、包含前瞻窗口的扩展观测空间，以及基于性能自动调整难度的目标课程学习。

Result: 训练出的策略可直接部署在配备机载传感器和计算单元的真实四足机器人上，表现出平滑转向、动量控制、提前面向未来目标、规划高效路径等涌现行为。

Conclusion: SmoothTurn框架成功实现了四足机器人在高速奔跑时的敏捷顺序导航，通过前瞻性规划和动量管理显著提升了转向平滑性和整体导航效率。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots show great potential for valuable real-world applications such as fire rescue and industrial inspection. Such applications often require urgency and the ability to navigate agilely, which in turn demands the capability to change directions smoothly while running in high speed. Existing approaches for agile navigation typically learn a single-goal reaching policy by encouraging the robot to stay at the target position after reaching there. As a result, when the policy is used to reach sequential goals that require changing directions, it cannot anticipate upcoming maneuvers or maintain momentum across the switch of goals, thereby preventing the robot from fully exploiting its agility potential. In this work, we formulate the task as sequential local navigation, extending the single-goal-conditioned local navigation formulation in prior work. We then introduce SmoothTurn, a learning-based control framework that learns to turn smoothly while running rapidly for agile sequential local navigation. The framework adopts a novel sequential goal-reaching reward, an expanded observation space with a lookahead window for future goals, and an automatic goal curriculum that progressively expands the difficulty of sampled goal sequences based on the goal-reaching performance. The trained policy can be directly deployed on real quadrupedal robots with onboard sensors and computation. Both simulation and real-world empirical results show that SmoothTurn learns an agile locomotion policy that performs smooth turning across goals, with emergent behaviors such as controlling momentum when switching goals, facing towards the future goal in advance, and planning efficient paths. We have provided video demos of the learned motions in the supplementary materials. The source code and trained policies will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [110] [Consistent and Efficient MSCKF-based LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with Inferred Cluster-to-Plane Constraints for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12904)
*Jinwen Zhu,Xudong Zhao,Fangcheng Zhu,Jun Hu,Shi Jin,Yinian Mao,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无人机的高效紧耦合激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过滑动窗口共面约束和零空间投影提升一致性，采用并行体素数据关联和紧凑聚类到平面测量模型提高效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机导航需要高精度和鲁棒性，但现有激光雷达-惯性里程计系统在尺寸、重量和功率受限平台上存在估计不一致性和计算瓶颈问题

Method: 基于高效多状态约束卡尔曼滤波器框架，构建滑动窗口平面特征的共面约束，通过零空间投影消除特征参数依赖；引入并行体素数据关联和紧凑聚类到平面测量模型

Result: 方法在一致性和效率方面优于现有技术，在退化场景中具有更好的鲁棒性，通过无地图特性实现最低内存使用，可在资源受限嵌入式平台实时运行

Conclusion: 提出的框架为无人机提供了具有优越一致性和效率平衡的激光雷达-惯性里程计解决方案，特别适合尺寸、重量和功率受限平台

Abstract: Robust and accurate navigation is critical for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) especially for those with stringent Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. However, most state-of-the-art (SOTA) LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) systems still suffer from estimation inconsistency and computational bottlenecks when deployed on such platforms. To address these issues, this paper proposes a consistent and efficient tightly-coupled LIO framework tailored for UAVs. Within the efficient Multi-State Constraint Kalman Filter (MSCKF) framework, we build coplanar constraints inferred from planar features observed across a sliding window. By applying null-space projection to sliding-window coplanar constraints, we eliminate the direct dependency on feature parameters in the state vector, thereby mitigating overconfidence and improving consistency. More importantly, to further boost the efficiency, we introduce a parallel voxel-based data association and a novel compact cluster-to-plane measurement model. This compact measurement model losslessly reduces observation dimensionality and significantly accelerating the update process. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms most state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches by providing a superior balance of consistency and efficiency. It exhibits improved robustness in degenerate scenarios, achieves the lowest memory usage via its map-free nature, and runs in real-time on resource-constrained embedded platforms (e.g., NVIDIA Jetson TX2).

</details>


### [111] [GoalSwarm: Multi-UAV Semantic Coordination for Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12908)
*MoniJesu Wonders James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalSwarm是一个完全去中心化的多无人机框架，用于零样本语义目标导航，通过轻量级2D语义占据地图、零样本基础模型和贝叶斯价值图实现开放词汇目标识别与高效协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人团队在未知环境中进行协作视觉语义导航面临两大挑战：重型感知模型在机载设备上的计算限制，以及去中心化多智能体协调的复杂性。现有方法难以实现鲁棒的开放词汇目标导航。

Method: 1) 使用SAM3零样本基础模型进行开放词汇检测和像素级分割；2) 构建贝叶斯价值图，融合多视角检测置信度形成像素级目标相关性分布；3) 采用去中心化协调策略，包括语义边界提取、基于测地线路径成本的效用竞标和空间分离惩罚。

Result: GoalSwarm框架实现了零样本语义对象目标导航，通过轻量级2D语义占据地图避免了完整3D表示的计算负担，同时保持了必要的几何和语义结构，能够有效协调多无人机进行协同探索。

Conclusion: GoalSwarm为多无人机系统提供了一种计算高效、完全去中心化的开放词汇语义导航解决方案，通过结合零样本基础模型、贝叶斯融合和智能协调策略，解决了重型感知模型部署和多智能体协调的关键挑战。

Abstract: Cooperative visual semantic navigation is a foundational capability for aerial robot teams operating in unknown environments. However, achieving robust open-vocabulary object-goal navigation remains challenging due to the computational constraints of deploying heavy perception models onboard and the complexity of decentralized multi-agent coordination. We present GoalSwarm, a fully decentralized multi-UAV framework for zero-shot semantic object-goal navigation. Each UAV collaboratively constructs a shared, lightweight 2D top-down semantic occupancy map by projecting depth observations from aerial vantage points, eliminating the computational burden of full 3D representations while preserving essential geometric and semantic structure. The core contributions of GoalSwarm are threefold: (1) integration of zero-shot foundation model -- SAM3 for open vocabulary detection and pixel-level segmentation, enabling open-vocabulary target identification without task-specific training; (2) a Bayesian Value Map that fuses multi-viewpoint detection confidences into a per-pixel goal-relevance distribution, enabling informed frontier scoring via Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) exploration; and (3) a decentralized coordination strategy combining semantic frontier extraction, cost-utility bidding with geodesic path costs, and spatial separation penalties to minimize redundant exploration across the swarm.

</details>


### [112] [MotionAnymesh: Physics-Grounded Articulation for Simulation-Ready Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12936)
