*WenBo Xu,Liu Liu,Li Zhang,Dan Guo,RuoNan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionAnymesh：一个自动化零样本框架，将静态3D网格转换为可交互的铰接式数字孪生资产，通过物理接地消除运动学幻觉，确保无碰撞铰接


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本流水线在处理复杂资产时缺乏物理接地，导致运动学幻觉和网格穿透问题，限制了在具身AI和机器人仿真中的应用

Method: 1) 运动学感知部件分割模块：将VLM推理与显式SP4D物理先验接地；2) 几何-物理联合估计流水线：结合类型感知初始化和物理约束轨迹优化，确保无碰撞铰接

Result: 在几何精度和动态物理可执行性方面显著优于现有最先进基线，为下游应用提供高度可靠的资产

Conclusion: MotionAnymesh成功解决了静态网格到仿真就绪数字孪生的转换问题，通过物理接地方法有效消除了运动学幻觉并保证了无碰撞铰接

Abstract: Converting static 3D meshes into interactable articulated assets is crucial for embodied AI and robotic simulation. However, existing zero-shot pipelines struggle with complex assets due to a critical lack of physical grounding. Specifically, ungrounded Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently suffer from kinematic hallucinations, while unconstrained joint estimation inevitably leads to catastrophic mesh inter-penetration during physical simulation. To bridge this gap, we propose MotionAnymesh, an automated zero-shot framework that seamlessly transforms unstructured static meshes into simulation-ready digital twins. Our method features a kinematic-aware part segmentation module that grounds VLM reasoning with explicit SP4D physical priors, effectively eradicating kinematic hallucinations. Furthermore, we introduce a geometry-physics joint estimation pipeline that combines robust type-aware initialization with physics-constrained trajectory optimization to rigorously guarantee collision-free articulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MotionAnymesh significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric precision and dynamic physical executability, providing highly reliable assets for downstream applications.

</details>


### [113] [RoboStream: Weaving Spatio-Temporal Reasoning with Memory in Vision-Language Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12939)
*Yuzhi Huang,Jie Wu,Weijue Bu,Ziyi Xiong,Gaoyang Jiang,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Shuzhao Xie,Yue Huang,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboStream：通过时空融合令牌和因果时空图实现几何锚定与因果记忆的训练免费框架，显著提升长时程机器人操作可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM规划器将每个步骤视为孤立的观测-动作映射，缺乏时空推理能力，导致几何重建重复、动作后果遗忘、感知误差累积等问题，无法实现可靠的长时程操作

Method: 提出RoboStream框架：1) 时空融合令牌(STF-Tokens)将视觉证据绑定到3D几何属性实现持久对象锚定；2) 因果时空图(CSTG)记录动作触发的状态转换，保持因果连续性；无需额外训练或微调

Result: 在长时程RLBench上达到90.5%成功率，在真实世界积木搭建任务上达到44.4%，而SoFar和VoxPoser仅为11.1%，证明时空推理和因果记忆对可靠长时程操作至关重要

Conclusion: 时空推理和因果记忆是实现可靠长时程机器人操作的关键缺失组件，RoboStream通过几何锚定和因果连续性追踪机制显著提升了操作性能，为开放世界具身智能提供了重要进展

Abstract: Enabling reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation is a crucial step toward open-world embodied intelligence. However, VLM-based planners treat each step as an isolated observation-to-action mapping, forcing them to reinfer scene geometry from raw pixels at every decision point while remaining unaware of how prior actions have reshaped the environment. Despite strong short-horizon performance, these systems lack the spatio-temporal reasoning required for persistent geometric anchoring and memory of action-triggered state transitions. Without persistent state tracking, perceptual errors accumulate across the execution horizon, temporarily occluded objects are catastrophically forgotten, and these compounding failures lead to precondition violations that cascade through subsequent steps. In contrast, humans maintain a persistent mental model that continuously tracks spatial relations and action consequences across interactions rather than reconstructing them at each instant. Inspired by this human capacity for causal spatio-temporal reasoning with persistent memory, we propose RoboStream, a training-free framework that achieves geometric anchoring through Spatio-Temporal Fusion Tokens (STF-Tokens), which bind visual evidence to 3D geometric attributes for persistent object grounding, and maintains causal continuity via a Causal Spatio-Temporal Graph (CSTG) that records action-triggered state transitions across steps. This design enables the planner to trace causal chains and preserve object permanence under occlusion without additional training or fine-tuning. RoboStream achieves 90.5% on long-horizon RLBench and 44.4% on challenging real-world block-building tasks, where both SoFar and VoxPoser score 11.1%, demonstrating that spatio-temporal reasoning and causal memory are critical missing components for reliable long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [114] [Coordinated Manipulation of Hybrid Deformable-Rigid Objects in Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12940)
*Anees Peringal,Anup Teejo Mathew,Panagiotis liatsis,Federico Renda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于准静态优化的混合可变形线性物体操纵规划器，使用应变基Cosserat杆模型，在约束环境中处理刚柔混合装配体，通过解析梯度实现33倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操纵研究主要关注纯可变形线性物体（如绳索、电缆），但对于包含刚性和可变形部件的混合装配体在约束环境中的操纵仍然具有挑战性。需要开发能够同时处理刚性和可变形部件、利用可变形部件柔顺性通过约束的规划方法。

Method: 采用准静态优化框架，使用应变基Cosserat杆模型扩展刚体公式到混合可变形线性物体。开发了具有解析梯度的可微分模型，用于逆运动静力学问题求解，比有限差分基线快33倍。使用IKS解热启动轨迹优化问题，并采用解析导数实现实际可行性。与基于采样的可行性规划器进行比较验证。

Result: 在多种hDLO系统的仿真中验证了算法有效性。在双臂机器人系统中对三连杆hDLO进行实验验证，平均变形误差约3厘米（可变形连杆长度的5%）。与基于采样的规划器相比，证明了所提优化规划器的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于应变Cosserat杆模型的准静态优化规划器能够有效处理约束环境中的刚柔混合装配体操纵问题。解析梯度显著加速了逆运动静力学求解，使轨迹优化实际可行。该方法为复杂约束环境中的混合装配体机器人操纵提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Coordinated robotic manipulation of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as ropes and cables, has been widely studied; however, handling hybrid assemblies composed of both deformable and rigid elements in constrained environments remains challenging. This work presents a quasi-static optimization-based manipulation planner that employs a strain-based Cosserat rod model, extending rigid-body formulations to hybrid deformable linear objects (hDLO). The proposed planner exploits the compliance of deformable links to maneuver through constraints while achieving task-space objectives for the object that are unreachable with rigid tools. By leveraging a differentiable model with analytically derived gradients, the method achieves up to a 33x speedup over finite-difference baselines for inverse kinetostatic(IKS) problems. Furthermore, the subsequent trajectory optimization problem, warm-started using the IKS solution, is only practically realizable via analytical derivatives. The proposed algorithm is validated in simulation on various hDLO systems and experimentally on a three-link hDLO manipulated in a constrained environment using a dual-arm robotic system. Experimental results confirm the planner's accuracy, yielding an average deformation error of approximately 3 cm (5% of the deformable link length) between the desired and measured marker positions. Finally, the proposed optimal planner is compared against a sampling-based feasibility planner adapted to the strain-based formulation. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of the proposed approach for robotic manipulation of hybrid assemblies in constrained environments.

</details>


### [115] [ReMem-VLA: Empowering Vision-Language-Action Model with Memory via Dual-Level Recurrent Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12942)
*Hang Li,Fengyi Shen,Dong Chen,Liudi Yang,Xudong Wang,Jinkui Shi,Zhenshan Bing,Ziyuan Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReMem-VLA：一种用于机器人控制的循环记忆视觉语言动作模型，通过可学习的查询机制解决传统VLA模型在需要历史上下文任务中的记忆限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言动作（VLA）模型通常基于马尔可夫假设，在处理需要历史上下文的任务时容易出错。现有方法要么从记忆库中检索（易受干扰项误导），要么扩展帧窗口（固定时间范围限制长期记忆保留）。需要一种能够有效聚合和维护相关上下文信息的方法。

Method: 提出ReMem-VLA模型，包含两组可学习查询：1）帧级循环记忆查询：在连续帧间传播信息以支持短期记忆；2）块级循环记忆查询：在时间块间传递上下文以实现长期记忆。这些查询通过端到端训练聚合和维护相关上下文。此外，引入"过去观察预测"作为辅助训练目标以增强视觉记忆。

Result: 在记忆中心的仿真和真实机器人实验中，ReMem-VLA在空间记忆、序列记忆、情景记忆、时间记忆和视觉记忆等多个维度表现出强大的记忆能力。显著优于无记忆VLA基线（π0.5和OpenVLA-OFT），并在记忆依赖任务上大幅超越MemoryVLA。

Conclusion: ReMem-VLA通过创新的循环记忆查询机制有效解决了VLA模型在机器人控制中的记忆限制问题，在不增加训练或推理成本的情况下实现了短期和长期记忆能力，为需要历史上下文的机器人任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models for closed-loop robot control are typically cast under the Markov assumption, making them prone to errors on tasks requiring historical context. To incorporate memory, existing VLAs either retrieve from a memory bank, which can be misled by distractors, or extend the frame window, whose fixed horizon still limits long-term retention. In this paper, we introduce ReMem-VLA, a Recurrent Memory VLA model equipped with two sets of learnable queries: frame-level recurrent memory queries for propagating information across consecutive frames to support short-term memory, and chunk-level recurrent memory queries for carrying context across temporal chunks for long-term memory. These queries are trained end-to-end to aggregate and maintain relevant context over time, implicitly guiding the model's decisions without additional training or inference cost. Furthermore, to enhance visual memory, we introduce Past Observation Prediction as an auxiliary training objective. Through extensive memory-centric simulation and real-world robot experiments, we demonstrate that ReMem-VLA exhibits strong memory capabilities across multiple dimensions, including spatial, sequential, episodic, temporal, and visual memory. ReMem-VLA significantly outperforms memory-free VLA baselines $π$0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT and surpasses MemoryVLA on memory-dependent tasks by a large margin.

</details>


### [116] [Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12967)
*Wuding Weng,Tongshu Wu,Liucheng Chen,Siyu Xie,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaDA框架使用语言作为语义桥梁连接视觉理解和动作控制，通过三个可解释的动作基元和解耦表示提升机器人操作的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高级视觉语言理解与低级动作控制之间的异质性挑战，现有方法在生成新颖或语义相关任务的鲁棒准确动作方面存在困难

Method: 提出语言接地的解耦动作表示框架，包含三个可解释动作基元（平移、旋转、夹持器控制），采用语义引导的软标签对比学习目标对齐跨任务动作基元，并使用自适应加权策略平衡对比学习和模仿学习目标

Result: 在LIBERO和MimicGen模拟基准测试及真实世界演示中，LaDA表现出强大性能，能有效泛化到未见或相关任务

Conclusion: LaDA框架通过语言接地的解耦动作表示成功连接感知与控制，提升了机器人操作的泛化能力和动作一致性

Abstract: The heterogeneity between high-level vision-language understanding and low-level action control remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Although recent methods have advanced task-specific action alignment, they often struggle to generate robust and accurate actions for novel or semantically related tasks. To address this, we propose the Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation (LaDA) framework, which leverages natural language as a semantic bridge to connect perception and control. LaDA introduces a fine-grained intermediate layer of three interpretable action primitives--translation, rotation, and gripper control--providing explicit semantic structure for low-level actions. It further employs a semantic-guided soft-label contrastive learning objective to align similar action primitives across tasks, enhancing generalization and motion consistency. An adaptive weighting strategy, inspired by curriculum learning, dynamically balances contrastive and imitation objectives for stable and effective training. Extensive experiments on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO and MimicGen) and real-world demonstrations validate that LaDA achieves strong performance and generalizes effectively to unseen or related tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Route Fragmentation Based on Resource-centric Prioritisation for Efficient Multi-Robot Path Planning in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12994)
*James R. Heselden,Gautham P. Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 资源中心的多机器人路径规划方法在农业走廊环境中通过路线分段实现高吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业环境中存在大量空间密集的导航瓶颈，现有以智能体为中心的多机器人路径规划方法从智能体角度解决冲突，而非从竞争资源角度，限制了空间交错能力

Method: 提出了两种基于优先级的片段规划器变体作为资源中心的MRPP算法，利用路线分段实现部分路线进展，限制基于二元等待的影响

Result: 在3.6公里拓扑地图的终身模拟中，片段规划器相比优先规划和基于优先级的搜索算法实现了显著的吞吐量提升，达到同期最优任务吞吐量的95%

Conclusion: 对于走廊主导的农业环境中农业机器人的长期部署，资源中心的多机器人路径规划方法是实现高效操作规划的必要条件

Abstract: Agricultural environments present high proportions of spatially dense navigation bottlenecks for long-term navigation and operational planning of agricultural mobile robots. The existing agent-centric multi-robot path planning (MRPP) approaches resolve conflicts from the perspective of agents, rather than from the resources under contention. Further, the density of such contentions limits the capabilities of spatial interleaving, a concept that many planners rely on to achieve high throughput. In this work, two variants of the priority-based Fragment Planner (FP) are presented as resource-centric MRPP algorithms that leverage route fragmentation to enable partial route progression and limit the impact of binary-based waiting. These approaches are evaluated in lifelong simulation over a 3.6km topological map representing a commercial polytunnel environment. Their performances are contrasted against 5 baseline algorithms with varying robotic fleet sizes. The Fragment Planners achieved significant gains in throughput compared with Prioritised Planning (PP) and Priority-Based Search (PBS) algorithms. They further demonstrated a task throughput of 95% of the optimal task throughput over the same time period. This work shows that, for long-term deployment of agricultural robots in corridor-dominant agricultural environments, resource-centric MRPP approaches are a necessity for high-efficacy operational planning.

</details>


### [118] [SldprtNet: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for CAD Generation in Language-Driven 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13098)
*Ruogu Li,Sikai Li,Yao Mu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SldprtNet是一个包含24.2万个工业零件的大规模多模态数据集，支持语义驱动的CAD建模、几何深度学习以及3D设计多模态模型的训练和微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏大规模、多模态的工业零件数据集来支持语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习。现有数据集通常规模有限、模态单一，难以满足现代CAD生成任务对多模态输入的需求。

Method: 1) 收集24.2万个工业零件，提供.step和.sldprt格式的3D模型；2) 开发编码器/解码器工具，支持13种CAD命令，实现3D模型与结构化文本表示的无损转换；3) 为每个样本生成7个视角的合成图像以减少输入token长度；4) 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B多模态模型生成零件的自然语言描述；5) 人工验证和校准描述、图像和3D模型的对齐。

Result: 构建了包含参数化建模脚本、渲染图像、3D模型文件和自然语言描述的完全对齐的多模态数据集。在数据集子集上微调基线模型，比较图像+文本输入与纯文本输入，结果证实了多模态数据集对CAD生成的必要性和价值。

Conclusion: SldprtNet是一个全面的多模态数据集，具有真实工业零件、支持工具、多样模态以及模型复杂度和几何特征的多样性，为语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习提供了重要资源。

Abstract: We introduce SldprtNet, a large-scale dataset comprising over 242,000 industrial parts, designed for semantic-driven CAD modeling, geometric deep learning, and the training and fine-tuning of multimodal models for 3D design. The dataset provides 3D models in both .step and .sldprt formats to support diverse training and testing. To enable parametric modeling and facilitate dataset scalability, we developed supporting tools, an encoder and a decoder, which support 13 types of CAD commands and enable lossless transformation between 3D models and a structured text representation. Additionally, each sample is paired with a composite image created by merging seven rendered views from different viewpoints of the 3D model, effectively reducing input token length and accelerating inference. By combining this image with the parameterized text output from the encoder, we employ the lightweight multimodal language model Qwen2.5-VL-7B to generate a natural language description of each part's appearance and functionality. To ensure accuracy, we manually verified and aligned the generated descriptions, rendered images, and 3D models. These descriptions, along with the parameterized modeling scripts, rendered images, and 3D model files, are fully aligned to construct SldprtNet. To assess its effectiveness, we fine-tuned baseline models on a dataset subset, comparing image-plus-text inputs with text-only inputs. Results confirm the necessity and value of multimodal datasets for CAD generation. It features carefully selected real-world industrial parts, supporting tools for scalable dataset expansion, diverse modalities, and ensured diversity in model complexity and geometric features, making it a comprehensive multimodal dataset built for semantic-driven CAD modeling and cross-modal learning.

</details>


### [119] [Evaluating VLMs' Spatial Reasoning Over Robot Motion: A Step Towards Robot Planning with Motion Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13100)
*Wenxi Wu,Jingjing Zhang,Martim Brandão*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理能力上的表现，分析不同查询方法的效果，发现Qwen2.5-VL在零样本情况下达到71.4%准确率，微调后可达75%，GPT-4o表现较差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解用户指令和物体空间关系对智能机器人系统至关重要。虽然视觉语言模型具有自然语言和空间推理能力，但尚不清楚其在机器人运动规划中处理空间约束（如距离偏好、拓扑属性、运动风格）的能力程度

Method: 评估四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理能力，使用四种不同的查询方法，评估两种运动偏好类型（物体接近度和路径风格），分析准确率与计算成本（token数量）的权衡

Result: 使用最佳查询方法时，Qwen2.5-VL在零样本情况下达到71.4%准确率，在较小模型微调后达到75%准确率，GPT-4o表现较差。研究还分析了准确率与计算成本之间的权衡关系

Conclusion: 这项工作显示了视觉语言模型与机器人运动规划管道集成的潜力，但当前模型在空间推理能力上仍有提升空间，需要进一步研究以提高其在实际机器人应用中的实用性

Abstract: Understanding user instructions and object spatial relations in surrounding environments is crucial for intelligent robot systems to assist humans in various tasks. The natural language and spatial reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have the potential to enhance the generalization of robot planners on new tasks, objects, and motion specifications. While foundation models have been applied to task planning, it is still unclear the degree to which they have the capability of spatial reasoning required to enforce user preferences or constraints on motion, such as desired distances from objects, topological properties, or motion style preferences. In this paper, we evaluate the capability of four state-of-the-art VLMs at spatial reasoning over robot motion, using four different querying methods. Our results show that, with the highest-performing querying method, Qwen2.5-VL achieves 71.4% accuracy zero-shot and 75% on a smaller model after fine-tuning, and GPT-4o leads to lower performance. We evaluate two types of motion preferences (object-proximity and path-style), and we also analyze the trade-off between accuracy and computation cost in number of tokens. This work shows some promise in the potential of VLM integration with robot motion planning pipelines.

</details>


### [120] [A Feasibility-Enhanced Control Barrier Function Method for Multi-UAV Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13103)
*Qishen Zhong,Junlong Wu,Jian Yang,Guanwei Xiao,Junqi Wu,Zimeng Jiang,Pingan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可行性增强控制屏障函数框架，解决多无人机密集场景中CBF-QP可行性问题，通过符号一致性约束缓解内部不兼容性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集多无人机场景中，多个CBF约束之间的内部不兼容性会损害CBF二次规划的可行性，影响碰撞避免性能

Method: 分析CBF约束的内部兼容性，推导内部兼容性的充分条件，引入符号一致性约束，结合最坏情况估计和松弛变量构建分散式CBF-QP

Result: 仿真结果表明，相比现有基线方法，所提方法在密集场景中显著减少不可行性并提升碰撞避免性能，在不同时间延迟下保持鲁棒性，真实实验验证了实际适用性

Conclusion: 提出的FECBF框架通过解决CBF约束的内部不兼容性问题，有效提升了多无人机密集场景中碰撞避免的可行性和性能

Abstract: This paper presents a feasibility-enhanced control barrier function (FECBF) framework for multi-UAV collision avoidance. In dense multi-UAV scenarios, the feasibility of the CBF quadratic program (CBF-QP) can be compromised due to internal incompatibility among multiple CBF constraints. To address this issue, we analyze the internal compatibility of CBF constraints and derive a sufficient condition for internal compatibility. Based on this condition, a sign-consistency constraint is introduced to mitigate internal incompatibility. The proposed constraint is incorporated into a decentralized CBF-QP formulation using worst-case estimates and slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces infeasibility and improves collision avoidance performance compared with existing baselines in dense scenarios. Additional simulations under varying time delays demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method. Real-world experiments validate the practical applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [121] [Panoramic Multimodal Semantic Occupancy Prediction for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13108)
*Guoqiang Zhao,Zhe Yang,Sheng Wu,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Yuanfan Zheng,Kai Luo,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个四足机器人全景多模态占据数据集PanoMMOcc及相应感知框架VoxelHound，通过垂直抖动补偿和多模态信息提示融合提升复杂环境下的占据预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据预测方法主要针对轮式自动驾驶设计，严重依赖RGB线索，在复杂环境中鲁棒性有限。四足机器人的运动特性和全景成像需求需要专门的数据集和感知框架。

Method: 1) 构建PanoMMOcc数据集，包含四种感知模态的多样化场景；2) 提出VoxelHound框架，包含垂直抖动补偿模块(VJC)缓解身体俯仰滚转引起的视角扰动，以及多模态信息提示融合模块(MIPF)联合利用全景视觉线索和辅助模态增强体素占据预测。

Result: 在PanoMMOcc基准上，VoxelHound实现了最先进的性能（mIoU提升4.16%）。数据集和代码将公开，促进具身机器人系统的全景多模态3D感知研究。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了四足机器人全景多模态占据感知的空白，提出的数据集和框架为复杂具身场景下的鲁棒感知提供了有效解决方案，推动了机器人3D感知技术的发展。

Abstract: Panoramic imagery provides holistic 360° visual coverage for perception in quadruped robots. However, existing occupancy prediction methods are mainly designed for wheeled autonomous driving and rely heavily on RGB cues, limiting their robustness in complex environments. To bridge this gap, (1) we present PanoMMOcc, the first real-world panoramic multimodal occupancy dataset for quadruped robots, featuring four sensing modalities across diverse scenes. (2) We propose a panoramic multimodal occupancy perception framework, VoxelHound, tailored for legged mobility and spherical imaging. Specifically, we design (i) a Vertical Jitter Compensation (VJC) module to mitigate severe viewpoint perturbations caused by body pitch and roll during mobility, enabling more consistent spatial reasoning, and (ii) an effective Multimodal Information Prompt Fusion (MIPF) module that jointly leverages panoramic visual cues and auxiliary modalities to enhance volumetric occupancy prediction. (3) We establish a benchmark based on PanoMMOcc and provide detailed data analysis to enable systematic evaluation of perception methods under challenging embodied scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VoxelHound achieves state-of-the-art performance on PanoMMOcc (+4.16%} in mIoU). The dataset and code will be publicly released to facilitate future research on panoramic multimodal 3D perception for embodied robotic systems at https://github.com/SXDR/PanoMMOcc, along with the calibration tools released at https://github.com/losehu/CameraLiDAR-Calib.

</details>


### [122] [DecoVLN: Decoupling Observation, Reasoning, and Correction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13133)
*Zihao Xin,Wentong Li,Yixuan Jiang,Bin Wang,Runming Cong,Jie Qin,Shengjun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeCoVLN是一个用于长视野视觉语言导航的框架，通过自适应记忆优化和状态-动作对级纠错微调来解决长期记忆构建和误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法面临两大挑战：构建有效的长期记忆库和克服误差累积问题。传统方法难以平衡语义相关性、视觉多样性和时间覆盖性，同时误差传播会严重影响长轨迹导航性能。

Method: 1. 将长期记忆构建形式化为优化问题，引入自适应细化机制，通过迭代优化统一评分函数从历史候选池中选择关键帧；2. 提出状态-动作对级纠错微调策略，利用状态间的测地距离精确量化与专家轨迹的偏差，在可信区域内收集高质量数据并过滤低相关性污染数据。

Result: 大量实验证明了DeCoVLN的有效性，该框架已在真实世界环境中部署应用，显著提升了长视野导航的鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: DeCoVLN通过创新的记忆优化和纠错机制，有效解决了视觉语言导航中的长期记忆构建和误差累积问题，为复杂3D环境中的长视野导航提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow long-horizon instructions and navigate complex 3D environments. However, existing approaches face two major challenges: constructing an effective long-term memory bank and overcoming the compounding errors problem. To address these issues, we propose DecoVLN, an effective framework designed for robust streaming perception and closed-loop control in long-horizon navigation. First, we formulate long-term memory construction as an optimization problem and introduce adaptive refinement mechanism that selects frames from a historical candidate pool by iteratively optimizing a unified scoring function. This function jointly balances three key criteria: semantic relevance to the instruction, visual diversity from the selected memory, and temporal coverage of the historical trajectory. Second, to alleviate compounding errors, we introduce a state-action pair-level corrective finetuning strategy. By leveraging geodesic distance between states to precisely quantify deviation from the expert trajectory, the agent collects high-quality state-action pairs in the trusted region while filtering out the polluted data with low relevance. This improves both the efficiency and stability of error correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DecoVLN, and we have deployed it in real-world environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [RXNRECer Enables Fine-grained Enzymatic Function Annotation through Active Learning and Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12694)
*Zhenkun Shi,Jun Zhu,Dehang Wang,BoYu Chen,Qianqian Yuan,Zhitao Mao,Fan Wei,Weining Wu,Xiaoping Liao,Hongwu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RXNRECer是一个基于Transformer的集成框架，直接预测酶催化反应而不依赖EC编号，通过整合蛋白质语言建模和主动学习，在多个指标上显著优于基于EC的基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酶注释方法主要依赖EC编号作为中间媒介，但这种方法存在多个问题：蛋白质、EC编号和反应之间存在复杂的多对多映射关系；EC编号频繁更新；不同数据库之间存在不一致性。这些因素导致间接预测策略引入模糊性，需要开发直接预测酶催化反应的方法。

Method: RXNRECer采用基于Transformer的集成框架，整合蛋白质语言建模和主动学习。蛋白质语言建模捕获高层次序列语义，主动学习捕获细粒度的转化模式。该方法不依赖EC编号作为中间媒介，直接预测酶催化反应。

Result: 在精心策划的交叉验证和时间测试集上评估，RXNRECer在六个基于EC的基准方法上取得一致改进：F1分数提升16.54%，准确率提升15.43%。框架还展现出下游应用优势：可扩展的全蛋白质组反应注释、增强通用反应模式的细化特异性、系统注释先前未策划的蛋白质、可靠识别酶的多功能性。

Conclusion: RXNRECer提供了一个稳健且多功能的解决方案，用于无EC的细粒度酶功能预测。通过整合大语言模型，它还能为预测提供可解释的理由。该方法在酶研究和工业应用的多个领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: A key challenge in enzyme annotation is identifying the biochemical reactions catalyzed by proteins. Most existing methods rely on Enzyme Commission (EC) numbers as intermediaries: they first predict an EC number and then retrieve the associated reactions. This indirect strategy introduces ambiguity due to the complex many-to-many mappings among proteins, EC numbers, and reactions, and is further complicated by frequent updates to EC numbers and inconsistencies across databases. To address these challenges, we present RXNRECer, a transformer-based ensemble framework that directly predicts enzyme-catalyzed reactions without relying on EC numbers. It integrates protein language modeling and active learning to capture both high-level sequence semantics and fine-grained transformation patterns. Evaluations on curated cross-validation and temporal test sets demonstrate consistent improvements over six EC-based baselines, with gains of 16.54% in F1 score and 15.43% in accuracy. Beyond accuracy gains, the framework offers clear advantages for downstream applications, including scalable proteome-wide reaction annotation, enhanced specificity in refining generic reaction schemas, systematic annotation of previously uncurated proteins, and reliable identification of enzyme promiscuity. By incorporating large language models, it also provides interpretable rationales for predictions. These capabilities make RXNRECer a robust and versatile solution for EC-free, fine-grained enzyme function prediction, with potential applications across multiple areas of enzyme research and industrial applications.

</details>


### [124] [Causal Cellular Context Transfer Learning (C3TL): An Efficient Architecture for Prediction of Unseen Perturbation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13051)
*Michael Scholkemper,Sach Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量级框架预测化学和遗传扰动对细胞状态的影响，无需大规模单细胞数据或大型基础模型，仅需广泛可得的批量分子数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测化学和遗传扰动对定量细胞状态的影响是计算生物学、分子医学和药物发现的核心挑战。现有方法依赖大规模单细胞数据和大型基础模型，但这些计算资源和数据集在学术或临床环境中并不总是可访问，限制了实用性。

Method: 利用生物干预的结构化特性和特定归纳偏置/不变性，开发轻量级扰动效应预测框架。该方法利用可获得的扰动效应信息实现对新环境的泛化，仅需广泛可得的批量分子数据。

Result: 通过与真实大规模干预实验比较，在预测环境特异性扰动效应方面表现出准确性。该方法与最先进的基础模型竞争，但需要更简单的数据、更小的模型规模和更少的时间。

Conclusion: 通过关注稳健的批量信号和高效架构，证明无需专有硬件或大型模型即可准确预测扰动效应，为生物医学中利用因果学习方法开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Predicting the effects of chemical and genetic perturbations on quantitative cell states is a central challenge in computational biology, molecular medicine and drug discovery. Recent work has leveraged large-scale single-cell data and massive foundation models to address this task. However, such computational resources and extensive datasets are not always accessible in academic or clinical settings, hence limiting utility. Here we propose a lightweight framework for perturbation effect prediction that exploits the structured nature of biological interventions and specific inductive biases/invariances. Our approach leverages available information concerning perturbation effects to allow generalization to novel contexts and requires only widely-available bulk molecular data. Extensive testing, comparing predictions of context-specific perturbation effects against real, large-scale interventional experiments, demonstrates accurate prediction in new contexts. The proposed approach is competitive with SOTA foundation models but requires simpler data, much smaller model sizes and less time. Focusing on robust bulk signals and efficient architectures, we show that accurate prediction of perturbation effects is possible without proprietary hardware or very large models, hence opening up ways to leverage causal learning approaches in biomedicine generally.

</details>


### [125] [From Garbage to Gold: A Data-Architectural Theory of Predictive Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12288)
*Terrence J. Lee-St. John,Jordan L. Lawson,Bartlomiej Piechowski-Jozwiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文挑战了"垃圾进，垃圾出"的机器学习格言，证明高维、共线性、有误差的表格数据通过数据架构与模型容量的协同作用，可以实现稳健预测，并提出了"主动数据中心AI"和"方法论迁移"的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决表格机器学习中的悖论：现代模型使用高维、共线性、有误差的数据却能取得最先进性能，这与传统"垃圾进，垃圾出"的理念相矛盾。需要从理论上解释这种稳健预测的来源，并为实际应用提供指导。

Method: 综合信息论、潜在因子模型和心理测量学原理，将预测器空间噪声划分为"预测器误差"和"结构不确定性"。证明高维误差预测器集能渐进克服两类噪声，而低维数据清洗存在根本限制。提出"主动数据中心AI"识别高效稳健的预测器，并推导系统误差机制的边界。

Result: 理论证明：高维误差预测器集能渐进克服预测器误差和结构不确定性；"信息性共线性"（来自共享潜在原因的依赖关系）增强可靠性和收敛效率；增加维度减少潜在推断负担，使有限样本下可行性成为可能。提出了"本地工厂"概念，支持从"模型迁移"到"方法论迁移"的部署范式转变。

Conclusion: 数据质量应从项目级完美重新定义为投资组合级架构。通过数据架构与模型容量的协同作用，即使使用"脏数据"也能实现稳健预测。这为从实时、未整理的"数据沼泽"中学习提供了理论基础，支持部署范式从"模型迁移"转向"方法论迁移"，以克服静态泛化能力的限制。

Abstract: Tabular machine learning presents a paradox: modern models achieve state-of-the-art performance using high-dimensional (high-D), collinear, error-prone data, defying the "Garbage In, Garbage Out" mantra. To help resolve this, we synthesize principles from Information Theory, Latent Factor Models, and Psychometrics, clarifying that predictive robustness arises not solely from data cleanliness, but from the synergy between data architecture and model capacity. Partitioning predictor-space "noise" into "Predictor Error" and "Structural Uncertainty" (informational deficits from stochastic generative mappings), we prove that leveraging high-D sets of error-prone predictors asymptotically overcomes both types of noise, whereas cleaning a low-D set is fundamentally bounded by Structural Uncertainty. We demonstrate why "Informative Collinearity" (dependencies from shared latent causes) enhances reliability and convergence efficiency, and explain why increased dimensionality reduces the latent inference burden, enabling feasibility with finite samples. To address practical constraints, we propose "Proactive Data-Centric AI" to identify predictors that enable robustness efficiently. We also derive boundaries for Systematic Error Regimes and show why models that absorb "rogue" dependencies can mitigate assumption violations. Linking latent architecture to Benign Overfitting, we offer a first step towards a unified view of robustness to Outcome Error and predictor-space noise, while also delineating when traditional DCAI's focus on label cleaning remains powerful. By redefining data quality from item-level perfection to portfolio-level architecture, we provide a theoretical rationale for "Local Factories" -- learning from live, uncurated enterprise "data swamps" -- supporting a deployment paradigm shift from "Model Transfer" to "Methodology Transfer'' to overcome static generalizability limitations.

</details>


### [126] [Multi-objective Genetic Programming with Multi-view Multi-level Feature for Enhanced Protein Secondary Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12293)
*Yining Qian,Lijie Su,Meiling Xu,Xianpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOGP-MMF：基于多目标遗传编程的蛋白质二级结构预测框架，通过多视图多级表示和特征融合优化，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质二级结构预测对理解蛋白质功能和药物发现至关重要，但复杂的序列-结构关系给准确建模带来挑战。现有方法在特征选择和融合方面存在不足，需要更有效的优化框架来平衡准确性和复杂性。

Method: 提出MOGP-MMF多目标遗传编程框架，将PSSP重新定义为特征选择和融合的自动化优化任务。采用多视图多级表示策略，整合进化视图、语义视图和新引入的结构视图。使用丰富的算子集演化线性和非线性融合函数，捕捉高阶特征交互同时降低融合复杂度。开发改进的多目标GP算法，引入知识转移机制利用先前的进化经验指导种群向全局最优收敛。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，MOGP-MMF在Q8准确率和结构完整性方面超越了最先进的方法。框架生成一组多样化的非支配解，为不同实际应用场景提供灵活的模型选择方案。

Conclusion: MOGP-MMF通过多视图特征表示和优化的多目标遗传编程，有效解决了蛋白质二级结构预测中的特征融合和准确性-复杂性权衡问题，为蛋白质功能研究和药物发现提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: Predicting protein secondary structure is essential for understanding protein function and advancing drug discovery. However, the intricate sequence-structure relationship poses significant challenges for accurate modeling. To address these, we propose MOGP-MMF, a multi-objective genetic programming framework that reformulates PSSP as an automated optimization task focused on feature selection and fusion. Specifically, MOGP-MMF introduces a multi-view multi-level representation strategy that integrates evolutionary, semantic, and newly introduced structural views to capture the comprehensive protein folding logic. Leveraging an enriched operator set, the framework evolves both linear and nonlinear fusion functions, effectively capturing high-order feature interactions while reducing fusion complexity. To resolve the accuracy-complexity trade-off, an improved multi-objective GP algorithm is developed, incorporating a knowledge transfer mechanism that utilizes prior evolutionary experience to guide the population toward global optima. Extensive experiments across seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that MOGP-MMF surpasses state-of-the-art methods, particularly in Q8 accuracy and structural integrity. Furthermore, MOGP-MMF generates a diverse set of non-dominated solutions, offering flexible model selection schemes for various practical application scenarios. The source code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/qian-ann/MOGP-MMF/tree/main.

</details>


### [127] [Synthetic Data Generation for Brain-Computer Interfaces: Overview, Benchmarking, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12296)
*Ziwei Wang,Zhentao He,Xingyi He,Hongbin Wang,Tianwang Jia,Jingwei Luo,Siyang Li,Xiaoqing Chen,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文综述了脑机接口中脑信号生成方法，系统分类了四种生成算法，并在四种代表性BCI范式上进行了基准测试，旨在解决神经记录数据稀缺、异构和隐私敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑机接口发展受限于神经记录数据的有限性、异构性和隐私敏感性，而生成合成且生理可信的脑信号成为缓解数据稀缺、增强模型能力的有前景途径。

Method: 系统地将现有生成算法分为四类：基于知识的方法、基于特征的方法、基于模型的方法和基于转换的方法；在四种代表性BCI范式上对现有脑信号生成方法进行基准测试。

Result: 提供了脑信号生成方法的全面分类和性能比较，公开了基准代码库（https://github.com/wzwvv/DG4BCI），为客观评估不同生成方法提供了标准框架。

Conclusion: 脑信号生成技术对构建准确、数据高效和隐私保护的BCI系统具有重要潜力，未来研究应关注生成方法的准确性、数据效率和隐私保护能力。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved transformative performance across diverse domains, largely driven by the large-scale, high-quality training data. In contrast, the development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) is fundamentally constrained by the limited, heterogeneous, and privacy-sensitive neural recordings. Generating synthetic yet physiologically plausible brain signals has therefore emerged as a compelling way to mitigate data scarcity and enhance model capacity. This survey provides a comprehensive review of brain signal generation for BCIs, covering methodological taxonomies, benchmark experiments, evaluation metrics, and key applications. We systematically categorize existing generative algorithms into four types: knowledge-based, feature-based, model-based, and translation-based approaches. Furthermore, we benchmark existing brain signal generation approaches across four representative BCI paradigms to provide an objective performance comparison. Finally, we discuss the potentials and challenges of current generation approaches and prospect future research on accurate, data-efficient, and privacy-aware BCI systems. The benchmark codebase is publicized at https://github.com/wzwvv/DG4BCI.

</details>


### [128] [Thermodynamics of Reinforcement Learning Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12324)
*Jacob Adamczyk,Juan Sebastian Rojas,Rahul V. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于非平衡热力学的几何框架，将强化学习中的课程学习形式化，通过最小化超额热力学功来寻找任务流形上的测地线作为最优课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用统计力学与机器学习的联系，特别是非平衡热力学的结果，为强化学习中的课程学习提供理论形式化框架，以更好地理解优化、泛化和表示学习。

Method: 将奖励参数解释为任务流形上的坐标，建立几何框架；通过最小化超额热力学功，证明最优课程对应于任务空间中的测地线；提出MEW算法用于最大熵强化学习中的温度退火调度。

Result: 建立了课程学习与热力学工作之间的理论联系，证明了最优课程对应于任务流形上的测地线，并开发了MEW算法用于温度退火调度。

Conclusion: 该工作为强化学习中的课程学习提供了基于热力学的几何形式化框架，将最优课程与任务流形上的测地线联系起来，并提供了实用的算法实现。

Abstract: Connections between statistical mechanics and machine learning have repeatedly proven fruitful, providing insight into optimization, generalization, and representation learning. In this work, we follow this tradition by leveraging results from non-equilibrium thermodynamics to formalize curriculum learning in reinforcement learning (RL). In particular, we propose a geometric framework for RL by interpreting reward parameters as coordinates on a task manifold. We show that, by minimizing the excess thermodynamic work, optimal curricula correspond to geodesics in this task space. As an application of this framework, we provide an algorithm, "MEW" (Minimum Excess Work), to derive a principled schedule for temperature annealing in maximum-entropy RL.

</details>


### [129] [Maximum Entropy Exploration Without the Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12325)
*Jacob Adamczyk,Adam Kamoski,Rahul V. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EVE算法，通过特征向量方法解决最大熵探索问题，避免昂贵的策略rollout和分布估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中高效探索是一个核心挑战，传统方法需要重复的策略rollout来估计状态访问频率，计算成本高昂。本文旨在寻找一种避免显式rollout的方法来解决最大熵探索问题。

Method: 采用内在平均奖励公式，将奖励定义为访问分布的函数，使最优策略最大化稳态熵。通过熵正则化目标的光谱特性，从问题相关转移矩阵的主特征向量计算稳态分布。提出EVE算法，使用类似基于价值方法的迭代更新计算解。对于原始非正则化目标，采用后验策略迭代方法单调改进熵并收敛。

Result: 证明了EVE在标准假设下的收敛性，实验表明EVE能高效产生具有高稳态熵的策略，在确定性网格世界环境中相对于基于rollout的基线方法实现了有竞争力的探索性能。

Conclusion: EVE算法通过特征向量方法有效解决了最大熵探索问题，避免了昂贵的策略rollout和分布估计，为强化学习中的探索问题提供了一种计算高效的新方法。

Abstract: Efficient exploration remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning, serving as a useful pretraining objective for data collection, particularly when an external reward function is unavailable. A principled formulation of the exploration problem is to find policies that maximize the entropy of their induced steady-state visitation distribution, thereby encouraging uniform long-run coverage of the state space. Many existing exploration approaches require estimating state visitation frequencies through repeated on-policy rollouts, which can be computationally expensive. In this work, we instead consider an intrinsic average-reward formulation in which the reward is derived from the visitation distribution itself, so that the optimal policy maximizes steady-state entropy. An entropy-regularized version of this objective admits a spectral characterization: the relevant stationary distributions can be computed from the dominant eigenvectors of a problem-dependent transition matrix. This insight leads to a novel algorithm for solving the maximum entropy exploration problem, EVE (EigenVector-based Exploration), which avoids explicit rollouts and distribution estimation, instead computing the solution through iterative updates, similar to a value-based approach. To address the original unregularized objective, we employ a posterior-policy iteration (PPI) approach, which monotonically improves the entropy and converges in value. We prove convergence of EVE under standard assumptions and demonstrate empirically that it efficiently produces policies with high steady-state entropy, achieving competitive exploration performance relative to rollout-based baselines in deterministic grid-world environments.

</details>


### [130] [Overcoming the Modality Gap in Context-Aided Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12451)
*Vincent Zhihao Zheng,Étienne Marcotte,Arjun Ashok,Andrew Robert Williams,Lijun Sun,Alexandre Drouin,Valentina Zantedeschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出半合成数据增强方法生成可验证的上下文，构建CAF-7M大规模数据集，证明上下文质量而非架构限制是上下文辅助预测的主要瓶颈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文辅助预测(CAF)有望整合领域知识和前瞻信息，但现有多模态模型常无法超越单模态模型，作者假设这是由于现有数据集中上下文质量差且难以验证

Method: 引入半合成数据增强方法，生成既描述时间动态又可验证与数值历史互补的上下文，从而大规模创建数据集，构建包含700万个上下文增强时间序列窗口的CAF-7M语料库

Result: 半合成预训练能有效迁移到真实世界评估，并显示出明确的上下文利用证据，上下文辅助预测的性能提升主要受数据集质量而非架构限制

Conclusion: 数据集质量而非模型架构是上下文辅助预测的主要瓶颈，通过高质量可验证上下文数据可以显著提升预测性能

Abstract: Context-aided forecasting (CAF) holds promise for integrating domain knowledge and forward-looking information, enabling AI systems to surpass traditional statistical methods. However, recent empirical studies reveal a puzzling gap: multimodal models often fail to outperform their unimodal counterparts. We hypothesize that this underperformance stems from poor context quality in existing datasets, as verification is challenging. To address these limitations, we introduce a semi-synthetic data augmentation method that generates contexts both descriptive of temporal dynamics and verifiably complementary to numerical histories. This approach enables massive-scale dataset creation, resulting in CAF-7M, a corpus of 7 million context-augmented time series windows, including a rigorously verified test set. We demonstrate that semi-synthetic pre-training transfers effectively to real-world evaluation, and show clear evidence of context utilization. Our results suggest that dataset quality, rather than architectural limitations, has been the primary bottleneck in context-aided forecasting.

</details>


### [131] [Probing Length Generalization in Mamba via Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12499)
*Jan Rathjens,Robin Schiewer,Laurenz Wiskott,Anand Subramoney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mamba模型在推理序列长度超过训练长度时性能下降，研究通过视觉重建任务揭示了其长度泛化问题，并提出长度自适应变体改进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba作为通用序列模型因计算复杂度低和性能接近Transformer而受到关注，但其在推理序列长度超过训练长度时性能会下降，需要研究这一现象的原因和改进方法。

Method: 使用受控视觉任务（从图像块序列重建图像），分析不同序列处理阶段的输出，揭示Mamba对训练序列长度分布的行为适应模式，并引入长度自适应变体Mamba。

Result: Mamba会根据训练时遇到的序列长度分布调整其行为策略，这些策略无法泛化到超出训练范围的长度；提出的长度自适应变体在训练序列长度范围内提高了性能。

Conclusion: 研究为Mamba的长度泛化问题提供了直观视角，并指出了改进架构的方向，长度自适应变体是改善长度泛化性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Mamba has attracted widespread interest as a general-purpose sequence model due to its low computational complexity and competitive performance relative to transformers. However, its performance can degrade when inference sequence lengths exceed those seen during training. We study this phenomenon using a controlled vision task in which Mamba reconstructs images from sequences of image patches. By analyzing reconstructions at different stages of sequence processing, we reveal that Mamba qualitatively adapts its behavior to the distribution of sequence lengths encountered during training, resulting in strategies that fail to generalize beyond this range. To support our analysis, we introduce a length-adaptive variant of Mamba that improves performance across training sequence lengths. Our results provide an intuitive perspective on length generalization in Mamba and suggest directions for improving the architecture.

</details>


### [132] [Byzantine-Robust Optimization under $(L_0, L_1)$-Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12512)
*Arman Bolatov,Samuel Horváth,Martin Takáč,Eduard Gorbunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Byz-NSGDM：一种针对(L₀,L₁)-光滑函数在拜占庭攻击下的分布式优化算法，结合动量归一化和最近邻混合的拜占庭鲁棒聚合，实现O(K^{-1/4})收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法在处理(L₀,L₁)-光滑函数（具有状态依赖梯度Lipschitz常数）时面临挑战，同时需要抵御拜占庭工作者的恶意攻击。需要设计既能处理非标准光滑性又能抵抗拜占庭攻击的鲁棒算法。

Method: 提出Byz-NSGDM算法，结合动量归一化与增强的拜占庭鲁棒聚合（采用最近邻混合NNM）。动量归一化处理(L₀,L₁)-光滑性，NNM增强的聚合机制抵御拜占庭攻击。

Result: 理论证明算法达到O(K^{-1/4})收敛率，拜占庭偏差与鲁棒系数和梯度异质性成正比。实验在异构MNIST分类、合成(L₀,L₁)-光滑优化和小型GPT字符级语言建模上验证有效性，对抗多种拜占庭攻击策略。

Conclusion: Byz-NSGDM成功解决了(L₀,L₁)-光滑函数在拜占庭攻击下的分布式优化问题，通过动量归一化和NNM增强的拜占庭鲁棒聚合实现了理论保证和实际有效性，对动量和学习率选择具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider distributed optimization under Byzantine attacks in the presence of $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness, a generalization of standard $L$-smoothness that captures functions with state-dependent gradient Lipschitz constants. We propose Byz-NSGDM, a normalized stochastic gradient descent method with momentum that achieves robustness against Byzantine workers while maintaining convergence guarantees. Our algorithm combines momentum normalization with Byzantine-robust aggregation enhanced by Nearest Neighbor Mixing (NNM) to handle both the challenges posed by $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness and Byzantine adversaries. We prove that Byz-NSGDM achieves a convergence rate of $O(K^{-1/4})$ up to a Byzantine bias floor proportional to the robustness coefficient and gradient heterogeneity. Experimental validation on heterogeneous MNIST classification, synthetic $(L_0,L_1)$-smooth optimization, and character-level language modeling with a small GPT model demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach against various Byzantine attack strategies. An ablation study further shows that Byz-NSGDM is robust across a wide range of momentum and learning rate choices.

</details>


### [133] [Curriculum Sampling: A Two-Phase Curriculum for Efficient Training of Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12517)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出课程采样方法，通过两阶段时间步采样策略解决Flow Matching模型中静态中间偏置采样导致的收敛速度与最终质量权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Flow Matching模型普遍采用静态中间偏置采样（如Logit-Normal），但研究发现这种选择会导致收敛速度与最终质量之间的权衡：中间偏置采样能加速早期收敛，但渐进保真度不如均匀采样。

Method: 通过分析每个时间步的训练损失，发现U形难度分布，边界区域存在持续误差。基于此提出课程采样：两阶段调度策略，第一阶段使用中间偏置采样进行快速结构学习，第二阶段切换到均匀采样进行边界细化。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，课程采样将最佳FID从3.85（均匀采样）提升到3.22，并在10万训练步而非15万步时达到峰值性能。

Conclusion: 时间步采样应被视为演化课程而非固定超参数，动态调整采样策略能显著提升Flow Matching模型的训练效率和最终质量。

Abstract: Timestep sampling $p(t)$ is a central design choice in Flow Matching models, yet common practice increasingly favors static middle-biased distributions (e.g., Logit-Normal). We show that this choice induces a speed--quality trade-off: middle-biased sampling accelerates early convergence but yields worse asymptotic fidelity than Uniform sampling. By analyzing per-timestep training losses, we identify a U-shaped difficulty profile with persistent errors near the boundary regimes, implying that under-sampling the endpoints leaves fine details unresolved. Guided by this insight, we propose \textbf{Curriculum Sampling}, a two-phase schedule that begins with middle-biased sampling for rapid structure learning and then switches to Uniform sampling for boundary refinement. On CIFAR-10, Curriculum Sampling improves the best FID from $3.85$ (Uniform) to $3.22$ while reaching peak performance at $100$k rather than $150$k training steps. Our results highlight that timestep sampling should be treated as an evolving curriculum rather than a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [134] [When LLM Judge Scores Look Good but Best-of-N Decisions Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12520)
*Eddie Landesberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，使用大语言模型作为评判者时，仅依赖全局相关性指标（如与参考标签的相关系数）会误导对模型在最佳选择任务中性能的评估，因为全局相关性主要由提示级别的基线效应驱动，而实际选择任务依赖于提示内的排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型常被用作评判者来给候选回答打分，并通过单一全局指标（如与参考标签的相关性）进行验证。这种评估方式具有误导性，因为实际部署任务通常是在同一提示下从多个候选回答中选择最佳回答（best-of-n选择）。全局相关性可能无法准确反映模型在提示内排序任务中的真实性能。

Method: 研究基于Chatbot Arena的5,000个提示构建了最佳选择基准（best-of-4）。分析全局相关性（r=0.47）与提示内相关性（r_within=0.27）的差异。考察粗粒度点式评分导致的平局问题（67%的成对比较出现平局）。在匹配对最佳选择审计（best-of-2）中，比较显式成对评判与点式评分的效果。

Result: 全局相关性为0.47的评判者仅能捕捉完美选择相比随机选择所能实现改进的21.0%。提示内相关性仅为0.27。点式评分导致67%的成对比较出现平局。在匹配对最佳选择审计中，显式成对评判将信号恢复率从21.1%提升至61.2%，显著改善了选择性能。

Conclusion: 对于基于评判者的选择任务，相关审计应报告提示内信号、平局率以及恢复率/前1准确率，而不仅仅是全局一致性。显式成对评判能显著提升选择性能，应替代单一全局相关性作为主要评估指标。

Abstract: Large language models are often used as judges to score candidate responses, then validated with a single global metric such as correlation with reference labels. This can be misleading when the real deployment task is best-of-n selection within a prompt.
  In a 5,000-prompt best-of-4 benchmark from Chatbot Arena, a judge with moderate global correlation (r = 0.47) captures only 21.0% of the improvement that perfect selection would achieve over random choice. The gap arises because global agreement is driven largely by prompt-level baseline effects, while selection depends on within-prompt ranking: within-prompt correlation is only r_within = 0.27, and coarse pointwise scoring creates ties in 67% of pairwise comparisons.
  In a matched-pair best-of-2 audit, explicit pairwise judging recovers much of this lost signal, raising recovery from 21.1% to 61.2%. For judge-based selection, the relevant audit should report within-prompt signal, tie rates, and recovery/top-1 accuracy, not global agreement alone.

</details>


### [135] [TERMINATOR: Learning Optimal Exit Points for Early Stopping in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12529)
*Alliot Nagle,Jakhongir Saydaliev,Dhia Garbaya,Michael Gastpar,Ashok Vardhan Makkuva,Hyeji Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TERMINATOR是一种用于大型推理模型的早期退出策略，通过预测首次答案出现位置来减少过度思考，在保持性能的同时显著缩短推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在复杂推理任务中经常出现过度思考问题，即使在生成最终答案后仍继续消耗计算资源。虽然存在最优推理长度，但确定这个长度对实际数据集来说非常困难，因为它完全依赖于任务和模型。

Method: TERMINATOR利用大型推理模型首次答案位置的可预测性，创建最优推理长度数据集来训练早期退出策略。该方法通过检测首次答案出现来预测何时可以安全地终止推理过程。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的实际数据集（MATH-500、AIME 2025、HumanEval、GPQA）上，TERMINATOR平均减少了14%-55%的推理长度，同时超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: TERMINATOR通过预测首次答案位置实现有效的早期退出，显著减少了大型推理模型的过度思考问题，在保持性能的同时大幅提高了推理效率。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve impressive performance on complex reasoning tasks via Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, which enables them to generate intermediate thinking tokens before arriving at the final answer. However, LRMs often suffer from significant overthinking, spending excessive compute time even after the answer is generated early on. Prior work has identified the existence of an optimal reasoning length such that truncating reasoning at this point significantly shortens CoT outputs with virtually no change in performance. However, determining optimal CoT lengths for practical datasets is highly non-trivial as they are fully task and model-dependent. In this paper, we precisely address this and design TERMINATOR, an early-exit strategy for LRMs at inference to mitigate overthinking. The central idea underpinning TERMINATOR is that the first arrival of an LRM's final answer is often predictable, and we leverage these first answer positions to create a novel dataset of optimal reasoning lengths to train TERMINATOR. Powered by this approach, TERMINATOR achieves significant reductions in CoT lengths of 14%-55% on average across four challenging practical datasets: MATH-500, AIME 2025, HumanEval, and GPQA, whilst outperforming current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [136] [A Reduction Algorithm for Markovian Contextual Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12530)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Osama Hanna,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将马尔可夫上下文线性老虎机问题简化为标准线性老虎机，通过构造平稳替代动作集和延迟更新方案控制非平稳分布偏差，匹配底层线性老虎机oracle的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有时间相关可用性的应用场景，将"上下文廉价"的简化视角从i.i.d.上下文扩展到马尔可夫上下文线性老虎机问题，其中动作集通过外生马尔可夫链演化。

Method: 在均匀几何遍历性条件下，构造平稳替代动作集并使用标准线性老虎机oracle求解；采用延迟更新方案控制非平稳条件上下文分布引起的偏差；对于未知转移分布的情况，提供分阶段算法在线学习替代映射。

Result: 获得高概率最坏情况遗憾界，与底层线性老虎机oracle的遗憾界匹配，仅对混合时间具有低阶依赖。

Conclusion: 成功将马尔可夫上下文线性老虎机问题简化为标准线性老虎机问题，实现了与i.i.d.情况类似的简化优势，同时有效控制了马尔可夫动态带来的额外复杂性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that when contexts are drawn i.i.d., linear contextual bandits can be reduced to single-context linear bandits. This ``contexts are cheap" perspective is highly advantageous, as it allows for sharper finite-time analyses and leverages mature techniques from the linear bandit literature, such as those for misspecification and adversarial corruption. Motivated by applications with temporally correlated availability, we extend this perspective to Markovian contextual linear bandits, where the action set evolves via an exogenous Markov chain. Our main contribution is a reduction that applies under uniform geometric ergodicity. We construct a stationary surrogate action set to solve the problem using a standard linear bandit oracle, employing a delayed-update scheme to control the bias induced by the nonstationary conditional context distributions. We further provide a phased algorithm for unknown transition distributions that learns the surrogate mapping online. In both settings, we obtain a high-probability worst-case regret bound matching that of the underlying linear bandit oracle, with only lower-order dependence on the mixing time.

</details>


### [137] [Embedded Quantum Machine Learning in Embedded Systems: Feasibility, Hybrid Architectures, and Quantum Co-Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12540)
*Somdip Dey,Syed Muhammad Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 嵌入式量子机器学习（EQML）旨在将量子机器学习能力引入资源受限的边缘平台，目前仅能通过混合工作流和嵌入式量子处理器概念实现，量子启发的经典算法是实用桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子机器学习能力扩展到资源受限的边缘平台（如物联网节点、可穿戴设备、无人机等），解决传统边缘AI系统的局限性，探索量子计算在边缘计算中的应用潜力。

Method: 从电路与系统角度分析可行性，形式化两种实现路径：1）混合工作流（边缘设备执行传感和经典处理，远程卸载量子子程序）；2）嵌入式量子处理器概念（紧凑型量子协处理器与经典控制硬件集成）。同时探讨量子启发的经典算法作为实用桥梁。

Result: 识别出主要障碍：延迟、数据编码开销、NISQ噪声、工具不匹配和能耗。将这些障碍映射到具体工程方向：接口设计、控制电子、电源管理、验证和安全。提出负责任部署需要对抗性评估和治理实践。

Conclusion: 嵌入式量子机器学习在2026年仅能以有限形式实现，量子启发的经典算法是当前实用桥梁。需要解决技术障碍并建立负责任部署框架，为边缘AI系统提供量子增强能力。

Abstract: Embedded quantum machine learning (EQML) seeks to bring quantum machine learning (QML) capabilities to resource-constrained edge platforms such as IoT nodes, wearables, drones, and cyber-physical controllers. In 2026, EQML is technically feasible only in limited and highly experimental forms: (i) hybrid workflows where an embedded device performs sensing and classical processing while offloading a narrowly scoped quantum subroutine to a remote quantum processing unit (QPU) or nearby quantum appliance, and (ii) early-stage "embedded QPU" concepts in which a compact quantum co-processor is integrated with classical control hardware. A practical bridge is quantum-inspired machine learning and optimisation on classical embedded processors and FPGAs. This paper analyses feasibility from a circuits-and-systems perspective aligned with the academic community, formalises two implementation pathways, identifies the dominant barriers (latency, data encoding overhead, NISQ noise, tooling mismatch, and energy), and maps them to concrete engineering directions in interface design, control electronics, power management, verification, and security. We also argue that responsible deployment requires adversarial evaluation and governance practices that are increasingly necessary for edge AI systems.

</details>


### [138] [As Language Models Scale, Low-order Linear Depth Dynamics Emerge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12541)
*Buddhika Nettasinghe,Geethu Joseph*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现GPT-2等大语言模型的深度动态可以用低阶线性替代模型准确描述，且模型规模越大线性特征越明显


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常被视为高维非线性黑箱系统，研究者希望探索其深度动态是否具有可解释的简单结构

Method: 构建32维线性替代模型来近似GPT-2-large的层间敏感度分布，通过加性注入实验验证线性替代模型的有效性，并在GPT-2系列模型中研究缩放规律

Result: 线性替代模型能近乎完美地复现GPT-2-large的层间敏感度分布；发现缩放原理：固定阶数的线性替代模型与完整模型的匹配度随模型规模单调提升；线性替代模型支持比启发式调度更节能的多层干预

Conclusion: 随着语言模型规模扩大，其上下文中的深度动态呈现出低阶线性特征，这为分析和控制大语言模型提供了系统理论基础

Abstract: Large language models are often viewed as high-dimensional nonlinear systems and treated as black boxes. Here, we show that transformer depth dynamics admit accurate low-order linear surrogates within context. Across tasks including toxicity, irony, hate speech and sentiment, a 32-dimensional linear surrogate reproduces the layerwise sensitivity profile of GPT-2-large with near-perfect agreement, capturing how the final output shifts under additive injections at each layer. We then uncover a surprising scaling principle: for a fixed-order linear surrogate, agreement with the full model improves monotonically with model size across the GPT-2 family. This linear surrogate also enables principled multi-layer interventions that require less energy than standard heuristic schedules when applied to the full model. Together, our results reveal that as language models scale, low-order linear depth dynamics emerge within contexts, offering a systems-theoretic foundation for analyzing and controlling them.

</details>


### [139] [Deep Distance Measurement Method for Unsupervised Multivariate Time Series Similarity Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12544)
*Susumu Naito,Kouta Nakata,Yasunori Taguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDMM是一种用于无监督多元时间序列相似性检索的深度距离测量方法，通过加权学习锚点-正样本对中的细微差异，提高工业场景下的检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业工厂中，用户关注时间序列中状态间的细微差异，现有方法难以有效学习这些细微差别，需要一种能够从整个时间序列中学习状态内细微差异的方法。

Method: DDMM使用一种学习算法，为从整个时间序列中任意采样的锚点-正样本对分配权重，权重基于对内的欧几里得距离，然后通过权重加权学习对内的差异，从而同时实现学习状态内细微差异和从整个时间序列采样。

Result: 在纸浆造纸厂数据集上，DDMM显著优于最先进的时间序列表示学习方法，证明了其在工业工厂中的有效性。通过与现有特征提取方法结合，可以进一步提高精度。

Conclusion: DDMM通过加权学习机制有效捕捉时间序列中的细微状态差异，在工业场景的无监督多元时间序列相似性检索中表现出优越性能，且可与现有特征提取方法结合获得更好效果。

Abstract: We propose the Deep Distance Measurement Method (DDMM) to improve retrieval accuracy in unsupervised multivariate time series similarity retrieval. DDMM enables learning of minute differences within states in the entire time series and thereby recognition of minute differences between states, which are of interest to users in industrial plants. To achieve this, DDMM uses a learning algorithm that assigns a weight to each pair of an anchor and a positive sample, arbitrarily sampled from the entire time series, based on the Euclidean distance within the pair and learns the differences within the pairs weighted by the weights. This algorithm allows both learning minute differences within states and sampling pairs from the entire time series. Our empirical studies showed that DDMM significantly outperformed state-of-the-art time series representation learning methods on the Pulp-and-paper mill dataset and demonstrated the effectiveness of DDMM in industrial plants. Furthermore, we showed that accuracy can be further improved by linking DDMM with existing feature extraction methods through experiments with the combined model.

</details>


### [140] [Asymptotic and Finite-Time Guarantees for Langevin-Based Temperature Annealing in InfoNCE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12552)
*Faris Chaudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论角度分析了对比学习中InfoNCE损失的温度参数动态，建立了对比学习与模拟退火之间的联系，证明了慢速对数逆温度调度能确保收敛到全局最优表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习中的InfoNCE损失严重依赖于温度参数，但其在固定与退火调度下的动态特性尚未得到充分理解。现有研究缺乏对这一关键参数调度策略的理论基础。

Method: 通过将嵌入演化建模为紧致黎曼流形上的朗之万动力学，在温和的光滑性和能量势垒假设下，将经典模拟退火理论扩展到对比学习设置中。

Result: 证明了慢速对数逆温度调度能确保以概率收敛到全局最优表示集合，而快速调度则可能陷入次优极小值。建立了对比学习与模拟退火之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和调优对比学习中的温度调度提供了理论基础，将模拟退火的收敛保证扩展到表示学习领域，为温度参数的选择提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: The InfoNCE loss in contrastive learning depends critically on a temperature parameter, yet its dynamics under fixed versus annealed schedules remain poorly understood. We provide a theoretical analysis by modeling embedding evolution under Langevin dynamics on a compact Riemannian manifold. Under mild smoothness and energy-barrier assumptions, we show that classical simulated annealing guarantees extend to this setting: slow logarithmic inverse-temperature schedules ensure convergence in probability to a set of globally optimal representations, while faster schedules risk becoming trapped in suboptimal minima. Our results establish a link between contrastive learning and simulated annealing, providing a principled basis for understanding and tuning temperature schedules.

</details>


### [141] [Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion LLMs with Entropy-Guided Step Selection and Stepwise Advantages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12554)
*Vishnu Teja Kunde,Fatemeh Doudi,Mahdi Farahbakhsh,Dileep Kalathil,Krishna Narayanan,Jean-Francois Chamberland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对扩散语言模型的无偏策略梯度方法，通过将去噪过程建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，避免了序列级似然评估，在编程和逻辑推理任务上取得SOTA结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法难以直接应用于扩散语言模型，因为扩散模型的序列级似然难以处理。现有方法依赖替代似然或启发式近似，这会引入偏差并模糊去噪的序列结构

Method: 将基于扩散的序列生成建模为去噪轨迹上的有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，推导出精确无偏的策略梯度，该梯度在去噪步骤上分解并用中间优势表示。采用熵引导的近似边界选择更新步骤，使用单步去噪奖励估计中间优势，避免昂贵的多步rollout

Result: 在编程和逻辑推理基准测试中展示了最先进的结果，在数学推理任务上表现出强大的竞争性能，优于现有的扩散语言模型RL后训练方法

Conclusion: 该方法为扩散语言模型提供了精确无偏的强化学习框架，避免了序列似然评估，在多个推理任务上实现了显著的性能提升

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been effective for post-training autoregressive (AR) language models, but extending these methods to diffusion language models (DLMs) is challenging due to intractable sequence-level likelihoods. Existing approaches therefore rely on surrogate likelihoods or heuristic approximations, which can introduce bias and obscure the sequential structure of denoising. We formulate diffusion-based sequence generation as a finite-horizon Markov decision process over the denoising trajectory and derive an exact, unbiased policy gradient that decomposes over denoising steps and is expressed in terms of intermediate advantages, without requiring explicit evaluation of the sequence likelihood. To obtain a practical and compute-efficient estimator, we (i) select denoising steps for policy updates via an entropy-guided approximation bound, and (ii) estimate intermediate advantages using a one-step denoising reward naturally provided by the diffusion model, avoiding costly multi-step rollouts. Experiments on coding and logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art results, with strong competitive performance on mathematical reasoning, outperforming existing RL post-training approaches for DLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/vishnutez/egspo-dllm-rl.

</details>


### [142] [A Spectral Revisit of the Distributional Bellman Operator under the Cramér Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12576)
*Keru Wang,Yixin Deng,Yao Lyu,Stephen Redmond,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了分布强化学习中贝尔曼算子在累积分布函数层面的内在结构，提出了正则化谱希尔伯特表示框架，将Cramér几何通过精确共轭实现而不改变底层贝尔曼动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布强化学习分析主要停留在度量层面，关注贝尔曼算子的收缩性质，但未能阐明贝尔曼更新在分布层面的结构作用。需要直接在累积分布函数层面分析分布贝尔曼动态，理解其内在的算子结构。

Method: 在累积分布函数层面分析分布贝尔曼动态，将Cramér几何作为内在分析框架。贝尔曼更新在CDF上表现为仿射作用，在CDF差异上表现为线性作用。基于此内在表述，构建了正则化谱希尔伯特表示族，通过精确共轭实现CDF层面的几何，而不修改底层贝尔曼动态。

Result: 揭示了分布贝尔曼更新在CDF层面的仿射和线性结构，其收缩性质为这种线性作用提供了均匀界限。正则化谱希尔伯特表示能够精确共轭Cramér几何，正则化仅影响几何结构，在零正则化极限下恢复原生Cramér度量。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了分布贝尔曼更新背后的算子结构，为分布强化学习中的函数分析和算子理论分析提供了基础，将分析从度量层面提升到结构层面。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) studies the evolution of full return distributions under Bellman updates rather than focusing on expected values. A classical result is that the distributional Bellman operator is contractive under the Cramér metric, which corresponds to an $L^2$ geometry on differences of cumulative distribution functions (CDFs). While this contraction ensures stability of policy evaluation, existing analyses remain largely metric, focusing on contraction properties without elucidating the structural action of the Bellman update on distributions. In this work, we analyse distributional Bellman dynamics directly at the level of CDFs, treating the Cramér geometry as the intrinsic analytical setting. At this level, the Bellman update acts affinely on CDFs and linearly on differences between CDFs, and its contraction property yields a uniform bound on this linear action. Building on this intrinsic formulation, we construct a family of regularised spectral Hilbert representations that realise the CDF-level geometry by exact conjugation, without modifying the underlying Bellman dynamics. The regularisation affects only the geometry and vanishes in the zero-regularisation limit, recovering the native Cramér metric. This framework clarifies the operator structure underlying distributional Bellman updates and provides a foundation for further functional and operator-theoretic analyses in DRL.

</details>


### [143] [CA-HFP: Curvature-Aware Heterogeneous Federated Pruning with Model Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12591)
*Gang Hu,Yinglei Teng,Pengfei Wu,Shijun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CA-HFP：一种面向异构边缘设备的联邦学习个性化压缩框架，通过曲率感知的剪枝和轻量级重构，在保持聚合兼容性和稳定收敛的同时显著降低计算和通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构边缘设备上的联邦学习需要个性化压缩，同时保持聚合兼容性和稳定收敛。现有方法在设备特定剪枝与全局模型聚合之间存在矛盾，需要一种既能适应设备异构性又能保持联邦学习收敛性的实用框架。

Method: 提出曲率感知异构联邦剪枝（CA-HFP）框架：1）每个客户端基于曲率信息的重要性评分进行设备特定的结构化剪枝；2）通过轻量级重构将紧凑子模型映射回全局参数空间；3）推导了考虑本地计算、数据异构性和剪枝扰动的联邦优化收敛界；4）基于此推导出有理论依据的损失函数剪枝准则。

Result: 在FMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上使用VGG和ResNet架构进行实验，在不同程度的数据异构性下，CA-HFP在保持模型精度的同时，显著降低了每个客户端的计算和通信成本，优于标准联邦训练和现有的剪枝基线方法。

Conclusion: CA-HFP为异构边缘设备联邦学习提供了一个实用的个性化压缩框架，通过曲率感知的剪枝和理论指导的优化，在保持模型性能的同时有效解决了计算和通信瓶颈问题，为资源受限环境下的联邦学习部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated learning on heterogeneous edge devices requires personalized compression while preserving aggregation compatibility and stable convergence. We present Curvature-Aware Heterogeneous Federated Pruning (CA-HFP), a practical framework that enables each client perform structured, device-specific pruning guided by a curvature-informed significance score, and subsequently maps its compact submodel back into a common global parameter space via a lightweight reconstruction. We derive a convergence bound for federated optimization with multiple local SGD steps that explicitly accounts for local computation, data heterogeneity, and pruning-induced perturbations; from which a principled loss-based pruning criterion is derived. Extensive experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 using VGG and ResNet architectures under varying degrees of data heterogeneity demonstrate that CA-HFP preserves model accuracy while significantly reducing per-client computation and communication costs, outperforming standard federated training and existing pruning-based baselines.

</details>


### [144] [Maximizing Incremental Information Entropy for Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12594)
*Jiansong Zhang,Zhuoqin Yang,Xu Wu,Xiaoling Luo,Peizhong Liu,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IE-CL是一种通过优化增强视图间的熵增益来改进对比学习的框架，在小批量设置下提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态增强和刚性不变性约束存在局限性，需要更灵活的信息理论框架来改进对比学习

Method: 将编码器视为信息瓶颈，联合优化两个组件：用于熵生成的可学习变换和用于熵保持的编码器正则化器

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、STL-10和ImageNet上，IE-CL在小批量设置下持续提升性能，核心模块可无缝集成到现有框架

Conclusion: IE-CL连接了理论原理与实践，为对比学习提供了新视角，通过显式优化熵增益来改进表示学习

Abstract: Contrastive learning has achieved remarkable success in self-supervised representation learning, often guided by information-theoretic objectives such as mutual information maximization. Motivated by the limitations of static augmentations and rigid invariance constraints, we propose IE-CL (Incremental-Entropy Contrastive Learning), a framework that explicitly optimizes the entropy gain between augmented views while preserving semantic consistency. Our theoretical framework reframes the challenge by identifying the encoder as an information bottleneck and proposes a joint optimization of two components: a learnable transformation for entropy generation and an encoder regularizer for its preservation. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100, STL-10, and ImageNet demonstrate that IE-CL consistently improves performance under small-batch settings. Moreover, our core modules can be seamlessly integrated into existing frameworks. This work bridges theoretical principles and practice, offering a new perspective in contrastive learning.

</details>


### [145] [Swap-guided Preference Learning for Personalized Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12595)
*Gihoon Kim,Euntai Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPL提出了一种新的偏好学习方法，通过虚构交换标注者和镜像偏好特性来防止后验坍塌，解决了VPL在稀疏偏好数据和过度表达解码器下忽略潜在变量的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF假设单一通用奖励，忽略了用户偏好多样性，限制了个性化。VPL引入用户特定潜在变量但存在后验坍塌问题，在稀疏偏好数据和过度表达解码器下会退化为单一奖励模型。

Method: 提出Swap-guided Preference Learning (SPL)，包含三个核心组件：1) 交换引导的基础正则化，2) 偏好逆自回归流(P-IAF)，3) 自适应潜在条件调节。通过构造虚构交换标注者并利用其偏好的镜像特性来指导编码器。

Result: 实验表明SPL能够有效缓解后验坍塌，丰富用户特定潜在变量表示，并提高偏好预测性能。代码和数据已开源。

Conclusion: SPL通过创新的交换引导机制解决了VPL的后验坍塌问题，为个性化偏好学习提供了更鲁棒的框架，能够更好地捕捉用户多样性偏好。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is a widely used approach to align large-scale AI systems with human values. However, RLHF typically assumes a single, universal reward, which overlooks diverse preferences and limits personalization. Variational Preference Learning (VPL) seeks to address this by introducing user-specific latent variables. Despite its promise, we found that VPL suffers from posterior collapse. While this phenomenon is well known in VAEs, it has not previously been identified in preference learning frameworks. Under sparse preference data and with overly expressive decoders, VPL may cause latent variables to be ignored, reverting to a single-reward model. To overcome this limitation, we propose Swap-guided Preference Learning (SPL). The key idea is to construct fictitious swap annotators and use the mirroring property of their preferences to guide the encoder. SPL introduces three components: (1) swap-guided base regularization, (2) Preferential Inverse Autoregressive Flow (P-IAF), and (3) adaptive latent conditioning. Experiments show that SPL mitigates collapse, enriches user-specific latents, and improves preference prediction. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/cobang0111/SPL

</details>


### [146] [Feynman: Knowledge-Infused Diagramming Agent for Scalable Visual Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12597)
*Zixin Wen,Yifu Cai,Kyle Lee,Sam Estep,Josh Sunshine,Aarti Singh,Yuejie Chi,Wode Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Feynman智能体用于可扩展的图表生成，通过知识组件枚举、代码规划和迭代优化，结合Penrose渲染系统创建视觉一致且多样化的图表，并构建了包含10万+图表-标题对的数据集和视觉推理基准Diagramma。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管互联网上图像和文本数据丰富，但知识丰富且对齐良好的图像-文本对仍然稀缺。视觉设计是多模态AI系统的关键应用，改进这些系统需要大规模的高质量视觉语言数据。

Method: Feynman智能体采用分阶段管道：1)枚举领域特定知识组件("ideas")；2)基于ideas进行代码规划；3)将ideas转换为简单声明式程序；4)迭代接收反馈并视觉优化图表；5)通过Penrose图表系统进行基于优化的渲染，保持视觉语义的同时注入随机性。

Result: 使用Feynman合成了包含超过10万个对齐良好的图表-标题对的数据集，并创建了视觉语言基准Diagramma用于评估视觉语言模型的视觉推理能力。该方法能以极低的成本和时间生成图表及其基础标题。

Conclusion: Feynman提供了一个可扩展的图表生成管道，能够高效创建视觉一致且多样化的图表，为多模态AI系统提供了高质量的训练数据和评估基准。计划将数据集、基准和完整智能体管道作为开源项目发布。

Abstract: Visual design is an essential application of state-of-the-art multi-modal AI systems. Improving these systems requires high-quality vision-language data at scale. Despite the abundance of internet image and text data, knowledge-rich and well-aligned image-text pairs are rare. In this paper, we present a scalable diagram generation pipeline built with our agent, Feynman. To create diagrams, Feynman first enumerates domain-specific knowledge components (''ideas'') and performs code planning based on the ideas. Given the plan, Feynman translates ideas into simple declarative programs and iterates to receives feedback and visually refine diagrams. Finally, the declarative programs are rendered by the Penrose diagramming system. The optimization-based rendering of Penrose preserves the visual semantics while injecting fresh randomness into the layout, thereby producing diagrams with visual consistency and diversity. As a result, Feynman can author diagrams along with grounded captions with very little cost and time. Using Feynman, we synthesized a dataset with more than 100k well-aligned diagram-caption pairs. We also curate a visual-language benchmark, Diagramma, from freshly generated data. Diagramma can be used for evaluating the visual reasoning capabilities of vision-language models. We plan to release the dataset, benchmark, and the full agent pipeline as an open-source project.

</details>


### [147] [FastDSAC: Unlocking the Potential of Maximum Entropy RL in High-Dimensional Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12612)
*Jun Xue,Junze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Shanze Wang,Yanjun Chen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastDSAC框架通过维度熵调制和连续分布评论家，解决了最大熵强化学习在高维人形控制中的探索效率问题，在复杂连续控制任务上超越了确定性策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决最大熵强化学习在高维人形控制中的挑战，特别是"维度诅咒"导致的探索效率低下和训练不稳定问题。挑战当前主流的高通量范式主要依赖确定性策略梯度与大规模并行仿真的妥协方案。

Method: 提出FastDSAC框架：1) 维度熵调制(DEM)动态重新分配探索预算并强制多样性；2) 连续分布评论家确保价值保真度并缓解高维价值高估问题。

Result: 在HumanoidBench和其他连续控制任务上的广泛评估表明，精心设计的随机策略能够持续匹配或超越确定性基线，在挑战性任务Basketball和Balance Hard上分别获得180%和400%的显著增益。

Conclusion: 通过FastDSAC框架证明，经过严格设计的随机策略在复杂连续控制中能够有效发挥最大熵强化学习的潜力，挑战了当前依赖确定性策略的主流范式。

Abstract: Scaling Maximum Entropy Reinforcement Learning (RL) to high-dimensional humanoid control remains a formidable challenge, as the ``curse of dimensionality'' induces severe exploration inefficiency and training instability in expansive action spaces. Consequently, recent high-throughput paradigms have largely converged on deterministic policy gradients combined with massive parallel simulation. We challenge this compromise with FastDSAC, a framework that effectively unlocks the potential of maximum entropy stochastic policies for complex continuous control. We introduce Dimension-wise Entropy Modulation (DEM) to dynamically redistribute the exploration budget and enforce diversity, alongside a continuous distributional critic tailored to ensure value fidelity and mitigate high-dimensional value overestimation. Extensive evaluations on HumanoidBench and other continuous control tasks demonstrate that rigorously designed stochastic policies can consistently match or outperform deterministic baselines, achieving notable gains of 180\% and 400\% on the challenging \textit{Basketball} and \textit{Balance Hard} tasks.

</details>


### [148] [When Drafts Evolve: Speculative Decoding Meets Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12617)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Hao-Cong Wu,Yichao Fu,Hao Zhang,Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OnlineSpec：一个利用在线学习技术持续优化草稿模型的推测解码框架，通过反馈循环提升加速效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中草稿模型因容量有限难以准确近似目标模型分布，导致接受长度短和加速效果差。观察到推测解码过程自然提供了草稿与目标模型偏差的验证反馈，形成了"草稿提交-反馈提供-草稿适应"的迭代循环，这与在线学习范式完美匹配。

Method: 提出OnlineSpec统一框架，基于动态遗憾最小化建立在线学习性能与推测系统加速率的正式联系。采用现代在线学习技术：1) 乐观在线学习，自适应重用历史梯度作为预测更新提示；2) 在线集成学习，动态维护多个草稿模型。

Result: 算法具有理论保证和改进的加速率，在七个基准测试和三个基础模型上实现了高达24%的加速。

Conclusion: 通过将推测解码重新构想为在线学习问题，OnlineSpec框架能够系统利用交互反馈持续演化草稿模型，显著提升大语言模型推理加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has emerged as a widely adopted paradigm for accelerating large language model inference, where a lightweight draft model rapidly generates candidate tokens that are then verified in parallel by a larger target model. However, due to limited model capacity, drafts often struggle to approximate the target distribution, resulting in shorter acceptance lengths and diminished speedup. A key yet under-explored observation is that speculative decoding inherently provides verification feedback that quantifies the deviation between the draft and target models at no additional cost. This process naturally forms an iterative "draft commits-feedback provides-draft adapts" evolving loop, which precisely matches the online learning paradigm. Motivated by this connection, we propose OnlineSpec, a unified framework that systematically leverages interactive feedback to continuously evolve draft models. Grounded in dynamic regret minimization, we establish a formal link between online learning performance and speculative system's acceleration rate, and develop novel algorithms via modern online learning techniques, including optimistic online learning that adaptively reuses historical gradients as predictive update hints, and online ensemble learning that dynamically maintains multiple draft models. Our algorithms are equipped with theoretical justifications and improved acceleration rates, achieving up to 24% speedup over seven benchmarks and three foundation models.

</details>


### [149] [Spend Less, Reason Better: Budget-Aware Value Tree Search for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12634)
*Yushu Li,Wenlong Deng,Jiajin Li,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BAVT是一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过动态搜索树和预算感知节点选择机制，在有限计算资源下优化LLM智能体的多跳推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前测试时扩展方法将计算视为无限资源，导致智能体在冗余步骤或死胡同轨迹上浪费token和工具预算。现有预算感知方法要么需要昂贵的微调，要么依赖粗糙的轨迹级启发式方法，无法在推理过程中进行干预。

Method: BAVT将多跳推理建模为动态搜索树，使用单一LLM主干进行步骤级价值估计。核心创新包括：1）预算条件节点选择机制，将剩余资源比例作为节点价值的自然缩放指数；2）残差价值预测器，评估相对进展而非绝对状态质量，以应对LLM自评估的过度自信问题。

Result: 在四个多跳QA基准测试和两个模型系列上的广泛评估表明，BAVT始终优于并行采样基线。在严格低预算约束下，BAVT的性能超过基线在4倍资源分配下的表现，证明智能预算管理从根本上优于暴力计算扩展。

Conclusion: BAVT提供了一种无需训练、理论保证的推理时框架，通过预算感知的搜索策略显著提升LLM智能体在有限计算资源下的可靠性，为资源受限环境中的高效推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Test-time scaling has become a dominant paradigm for improving LLM agent reliability, yet current approaches treat compute as an abundant resource, allowing agents to exhaust token and tool budgets on redundant steps or dead-end trajectories. Existing budget-aware methods either require expensive fine-tuning or rely on coarse, trajectory-level heuristics that cannot intervene mid-execution. We propose the Budget-Aware Value Tree (BAVT), a training-free inference-time framework that models multi-hop reasoning as a dynamic search tree guided by step-level value estimation within a single LLM backbone. Another key innovation is a budget-conditioned node selection mechanism that uses the remaining resource ratio as a natural scaling exponent over node values, providing a principled, parameter-free transition from broad exploration to greedy exploitation as the budget depletes. To combat the well-known overconfidence of LLM self-evaluation, BAVT employs a residual value predictor that scores relative progress rather than absolute state quality, enabling reliable pruning of uninformative or redundant tool calls. We further provide a theoretical convergence guarantee, proving that BAVT reaches a terminal answer with probability at least $1-ε$ under an explicit finite budget bound. Extensive evaluations on four multi-hop QA benchmarks across two model families demonstrate that BAVT consistently outperforms parallel sampling baselines. Most notably, BAVT under strict low-budget constraints surpasses baseline performance at $4\times$ the resource allocation, establishing that intelligent budget management fundamentally outperforms brute-force compute scaling.

</details>


### [150] [LightMoE: Reducing Mixture-of-Experts Redundancy through Expert Replacing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12645)
*Jiawei Hao,Zhiwei Hao,Jianyuan Guo,Li Shen,Yong Luo,Han Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LightMoE提出专家替换压缩范式，通过参数高效模块替换冗余专家，以低训练成本恢复能力，在30%压缩率下性能与LoRA微调相当，50%压缩率下优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大语言模型部署受限于大量专家模块的内存需求，现有专家压缩技术存在不可逆知识损失或高训练开销的问题

Method: 提出专家替换范式，用参数高效模块替换冗余专家；LightMoE框架引入自适应专家选择、分层专家构建和退火恢复策略

Result: 30%压缩率下性能与LoRA微调相当；50%压缩率下优于现有方法，在五个任务上平均性能提升5.6%

Conclusion: LightMoE在内存效率、训练效率和模型性能之间实现了优越平衡，为MoE模型压缩提供了新范式

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) based Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance and computational efficiency. However, their deployment is often constrained by substantial memory demands, primarily due to the need to load numerous expert modules. While existing expert compression techniques like pruning or merging attempt to mitigate this, they often suffer from irreversible knowledge loss or high training overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel expert compression paradigm termed expert replacing, which replaces redundant experts with parameter-efficient modules and recovers their capabilities with low training costs. We find that even a straightforward baseline of this paradigm yields promising performance. Building on this foundation, we introduce LightMoE, a framework that enhances the paradigm by introducing adaptive expert selection, hierarchical expert construction, and an annealed recovery strategy. Experimental results show that LightMoE matches the performance of LoRA fine-tuning at a 30% compression ratio. Even under a more aggressive 50% compression rate, it outperforms existing methods and achieves average performance improvements of 5.6% across five diverse tasks.
